HOSTILE G11 CASES — BINDING
---------------------------
Permanent G11 hostile coverage includes:
  accepted A -> external atomic replacement B -> ordinary Save must not overwrite B;
  same-size + same-mtime different bytes -> strong content change;
  direct symlink target content change;
  direct logical symlink retarget/replacement;
  active file deletion -> buffer/session preserved;
  repeated events -> coalesced/no dialog storm;
  own Save -> no false external-change warning;
  Modified + Reload Cancel -> exact modified state preserved;
  Modified + Discard Changes and Reload -> explicit disk reacceptance;
  stale generation result -> discarded;
  older same-generation result with newer pending verification -> suppressed;
  slow observer -> GTK main loop remains responsive;
  max concurrent strong observations = 1.

SOURCE-FIRST CLASSIFICATION
---------------------------
Direct inspection of tests/desktop/scenarios/monitoring.py, StrongExternalFileMonitor and the
supplied Mousepad 0.7.0 / GNOME Text Editor / gedit sources proves two separate issues.

1. PERMANENT TEST TOPOLOGY DEFECT — SOURCE PROVEN

The PENDING_SUPPRESSES_OBSOLETE_RESULT regression was intended to prove an internal semantic
invariant:
  - older same-generation result is suppressed when newer verification is pending;
  - the newest result is delivered;
  - at most one strong observation executes concurrently;
  - the state machine converges to quiescence.

Instead, the test kept a real Gio.FileMonitor bound to next_path, mutated that real monitored file,
and immediately injected a second private synthetic _schedule_observation() request while asserting
an exact observer-call total. Real Gio event cardinality is backend-dependent and may legitimately
contain multiple events for one write. Therefore the regression mixed two independent interrupt
sources and treated raw callback cardinality as semantic truth.

Classification:
  PERMANENT_TEST_TOPOLOGY_DEFECT=YES_SOURCE_PROVEN
  EXACT_T480_EVENT_TRACE=NOT_CLAIMED
  BLIND_DIAGNOSTIC_RERUN_REQUIRED=NO

2. PRODUCT EVENT-FILTERING WEAKNESS — SOURCE PROVEN

Graphium treated G_FILE_MONITOR_EVENT_CHANGES_DONE_HINT as a generic independent external-change
interrupt. The supplied mature controls do not support that model:

  Mousepad:
    CHANGES_DONE_HINT is used only in a narrow delete/create settling condition; it is not a generic
    second modification signal.

  GNOME Text Editor:
    generic external-change scheduling is driven by CHANGED / DELETED / RENAMED and then coalesced;
    CHANGES_DONE_HINT is not treated as another ordinary modification.

Graphium already has its own application-level debounce and one-worker strong-observation lane.
Treating CHANGES_DONE_HINT as generic input can therefore create redundant follow-up observations
without adding disk truth.

Classification:
  GENERIC_CHANGES_DONE_HINT_OVERTRIGGER=YES_SOURCE_PROVEN
  PRODUCT_RUNTIME_DEFECT=NARROW_EVENT_FILTER_NOISE_OVERTRIGGER
  VALID_USER_VISIBLE_PRODUCT_FAIL_FROM_T480_RUN=NOT_PROVEN
  CORE_STRONG_MONITOR_ARCHITECTURE=KEEP
  WHOLESALE_REDESIGN=REJECT

ADOPT / ADAPT / REJECT / DEFER
------------------------------
ADOPT:
  mature state-based/coalesced external-change ownership;
  semantic convergence rather than raw Gio callback cardinality.

ADAPT:
  keep Graphium's stronger observe_document() + classify_fresh_observation() truth;
  keep Graphium's existing 120 ms application debounce and one-worker lane;
  isolate the internal pending/generation regression from real Gio delivery.

REJECT:
  generic CHANGES_DONE_HINT as an independent strong-observation interrupt;
  exact Gio callback-count assertions while a live monitor is active;
  timeout/sleep retries to make the failed regression pass;
  weakening SHA-256/object/topology/Guarded Save safety.

DEFER:
  Gio.FileMonitor.set_rate_limit() addition. GNOME Text Editor uses one, but Graphium already owns
  application-level debounce; no evidence currently requires a second rate limiter.

SOURCE-ONLY REPAIR
------------------
Only two paths are changed relative to failed tree 3e658918... before MO synchronization:

  graphium/adapters/gtk/external_monitor.py
    - remove generic CHANGES_DONE_HINT from _relevant_event();
    - retain CHANGED, DELETED, CREATED, ATTRIBUTE_CHANGED, MOVED, RENAMED, MOVED_IN, MOVED_OUT;
    - no change to strong truth, worker ownership, generation ownership, symlink topology, UI or
      GuardedFileWriter.

  tests/desktop/scenarios/monitoring.py
    - add an explicit permanent semantic guard that CHANGES_DONE_HINT is not generically relevant;
    - make the synthetic slow-observer/generation block use a deterministic StrongExternalFileMonitor
      subclass whose _create_monitors() returns no real Gio monitors;
    - preserve the earlier real-Gio checkpoints unchanged, so real initial binding, content change,
      own-save suppression, replacement, deletion and symlink behavior remain platform-bound tests;
    - preserve pending suppression, newest-result delivery, quiescence and max-concurrency semantic
      assertions without mixing in backend-dependent Gio event multiplicity.

No new framework, queue, worker abstraction, compatibility shim or Gxx-specific executable
validation universe is added.

REVISED SOURCE-FIRST CLASSIFICATION
-----------------------------------
The second FAIL falsifies the first causal explanation as sufficient.

The failing synthetic pending block already used a StrongExternalFileMonitor subclass whose
_create_monitors() returned an empty list. Therefore no real Gio.FileMonitor existed inside the
remaining pending-result block. Backend CHANGED + CHANGES_DONE_HINT multiplicity cannot be the direct
cause of this second failure there.

The mature-backed runtime narrowing that rejects generic CHANGES_DONE_HINT remains independently
correct and is retained. It is not reverted.

Source audit of StrongExternalFileMonitor again found:
  - one in-flight strong observer only;
  - one coalesced pending generation only;
  - no unbounded queue;
  - stale-generation result discard;
  - current same-generation result suppression when newer current work is pending;
  - one follow-up scheduling decision for current pending work;
  - accepted DocumentFileState remains immutable monitor truth;
  - no event type is accepted as disk truth;
  - GuardedFileWriter remains the sole physical writer.

No source-proven runtime defect justifies changing that state machine.

The permanent True-GTK regression itself was not deterministic: despite the no-Gio subclass, it
still combined real GLib.timeout_add(), GLib.idle_add(), threading.Thread, filesystem observation,
threading.Event synchronization, short wall-clock deadlines and exact call/result cardinalities.
That authority could not distinguish an internal state-machine defect from scheduler/executor/result-
delivery convergence timing.

Classification:
  FIRST_CAUSAL_DIAGNOSIS=INCOMPLETE
  CHANGES_DONE_HINT_FILTER=KEEP
  VALID_PRODUCT_FAIL=NOT_PROVEN
  STRONG_MONITOR_RUNTIME_REDESIGN_JUSTIFIED=NO
  PERMANENT_INTERNAL_STATE_MACHINE_ORACLE_DEFECT=SOURCE_PROVEN
  EXACT_FAILED_SUBCONDITION=NOT_LOGGED_AND_NOT_INVENTED

NET-REDUCTIVE PERMANENT-AUTHORITY REPAIR
----------------------------------------
User authorized the source-only repair. Product runtime is byte-identical to the second-fail tree.
Only permanent validation authority changes before this MO append:

  tests/behavioral/test_document_state.py
    - adds one scheduler-independent test of the real StrongExternalFileMonitor pending/delivery
      state machine;
    - imports the production monitor under a minimal fake gi.repository module so the semantic
      authority is headless and does not require a GTK display;
    - when one worker is marked in-flight, another request must become the single pending generation
      and must not schedule a second worker;
    - current result + newer current pending work is suppressed;
    - exactly one follow-up scheduling decision is requested;
    - pending and in-flight ownership are cleared by delivery;
    - latest current result with no newer pending work is delivered;
    - stale-generation result is discarded.

  tests/desktop/scenarios/monitoring.py
    - removes the synthetic same-generation pending/exact-cardinality block from True-GTK;
    - removes the fixed-duration heartbeat-count oracle and helper;
    - retains real GTK/Gio monitoring checkpoints for initial bind, strong content truth,
      Reload/rebind, own-Save suppression, replacement/Guarded-Save fail-closed, deletion and
      symlink target/retarget behavior;
    - retains one real GLib/thread boundary proof that a blocked strong observer does not block the
      GTK main loop, using one idle heartbeat rather than callback-rate/cardinality timing;
    - retains the final nonmodal/no-dialog-storm UI check.

No product file changed.
No new test framework, worker abstraction, queue, polling loop, rate limiter or compatibility layer
was added.

OPENING SOURCE-FIRST CLOSURE FINDINGS
-------------------------------------
The published G11 product already has exactly six top-level menus and most final-v1 behavior. Five
closure areas remained source-provable before implementation:

  1. Document -> Encoding / Line Endings explicit conversion was frozen in Menu Architecture R3
     but not implemented; Architecture section 7 still described bounded conversion as SHOULD.
  2. Normal desktop install projection/desktop metadata did not yet exist.
  3. Help target wording and implemented command labels differed textually.
  4. No common external FIRST_EDITABLE oracle yet existed for the four comparators.
  5. Roadmap status lines for G09/G11 were stale after their actual closures.

MATURE-SOURCE FALSIFICATION / DISPOSITION
------------------------------------------
Direct supplied source was used; web was used only to verify current AT-SPI API facts.

Mousepad 0.7.0:
  - `MousepadFile` stores line-ending/BOM state next to saved-state facts; changing line-ending/BOM
    is an unbuffered modification rather than text mutation.
  - process-isolated comparator mode is `--disable-server`, which switches the application to
    non-unique mode.
  Decision: ADAPT representation-as-document-state and exact isolated comparator launch; REJECT
  Mousepad's weaker modified-flag model and weaker file-truth model.

gedit:
  - Save As carries explicit chosen encoding and newline type separately from editor text.
  Decision: ADOPT explicit representation choice at persistence boundary; ADAPT to Graphium's one
  GuardedFileWriter and exact state-ID relation.

Leafpad / L3afpad:
  - editor text is normalized internally while charset/line-ending file state controls serialization.
  Decision: ADOPT the small representation-state model; REJECT encoding guessing/fallback.

FeatherPad:
  - exact isolated comparator launch is `--standalone`; mature desktop source also demonstrates the
  value of ordinary desktop integration without making those facilities editor-runtime authorities.
  Decision: ADOPT comparator isolation and ordinary desktop launch metadata; REJECT its broader
  tab/session/code-editor surface.

AT-SPI 2.0 current API verification:
  - Accessible exposes `get_process_id()` for exact process ownership.
  - `generate_keyboard_event()` can synthesize keyboard input in the current UI context.
  These two facts permit a common external first-input-acceptance oracle without clipboard or Save.

REPRESENTATION CONTRACT FREEZE
------------------------------
G12 resolves the v1 SHOULD/MUST tension: bounded explicit representation conversion is now a v1 MUST
because the six-menu architecture already freezes Encoding and Line Endings under Document.

There is still exactly one document dirty authority. G12 extends the final relation from text-only
state identity to one composite relation:

  SAVED iff
    current_editor_state_id == saved_editor_state_id != None
    AND current_representation_profile == saved_representation_profile

Any other relation is Modified. This is not a second dirty boolean.

Frozen behavior:
  - Open: current=saved profile from accepted disk load.
  - New: current=saved UTF-8/no-BOM/LF.
  - explicit representation command changes current profile only; no text mutation, no text Undo,
    no writer call, no accepted-baseline mutation.
  - changing back to the exact saved profile can naturally restore Saved if text is also at savepoint.
  - Undo/Redo changes text history only and does not silently undo representation selection.
  - Save/Save As capture text state + representation state and may accept only that exact pair.
  - late save after newer text/representation change leaves Modified.
  - Save a Copy / Save Version Copy use current representation but never advance active savepoint.
  - fresh post-commit disk observation is truth; no fictitious representation survives when bytes
    cannot encode it as an observable property.

Frozen encoding choices:
  UTF-8 (no BOM)
  UTF-8 with BOM
  UTF-16 LE with BOM
  UTF-16 BE with BOM
  UTF-32 LE with BOM
  UTF-32 BE with BOM

Forbidden:
  legacy/locale codec guessing; UTF-16/32 without BOM; replacement-character fallback; implicit
  Save conversion.

Frozen line-ending choices:
  LF
  CRLF
  CR

Mixed EOL remains observation only. Selecting any concrete line-ending target on mixed input is
explicit normalization consent and clears the pending mixed-source condition. Encoding change alone
does not grant that consent.

Menu presentation separates observation from action:
  Encoding -> Current: <representation> [non-action] + explicit Convert to ... actions
  Line Endings -> Current: <representation> [non-action] + explicit Convert to ... actions

INSTALL / DESKTOP COMPLETENESS FREEZE
-------------------------------------
G12 will not add a generic packaging framework. One small standard-library installer in `bin/` will
own prefix/staging install. Default prefix is `~/.local`; explicit `--prefix` and `--destdir` support
system/package staging. Installed projection is product-only:

  PREFIX/lib/graphium/graphium/**
  PREFIX/lib/graphium/bin/graphium
  PREFIX/lib/graphium/docs/user/**
  PREFIX/bin/graphium -> private runtime launcher
  PREFIX/share/applications/io.github.leviagravia.Graphium.desktop

The desktop entry uses `Exec=graphium %F`, `MimeType=text/plain`, `Terminal=false`, Utility/TextEditor
categories and the generic `accessories-text-editor` icon. Tests, evidence, canonical development
documents, selftest and qualification laboratory are forbidden from installed payload.

AppStream/metainfo is DEFERRED because no project-license authority is frozen; legal metadata will not
be guessed merely to imitate mature projects. This does not block v1 launching/install behavior.

HELP / COMMAND AUTHORITY FREEZE
-------------------------------
The lower-churn implemented wording wins after mature/minimalism review:
  User Guide
  Keyboard Shortcuts
  About

Earlier roadmap wording `Graphium Help` / `About Graphium` is superseded. The Help menu context and
About dialog already identify the product, so repeating the product name adds no utility.

COMMON EXTERNAL FIRST_EDITABLE FREEZE
-------------------------------------
The common oracle is qualification-only AT-SPI 2.0 first-input acceptance, not product runtime.
For each 0 B / 5 KiB / 1 MiB / 10 MiB named disposable fixture:

  - isolated HOME/XDG;
  - exact spawned PID required;
  - Graphium normal launch; Leafpad/L3afpad normal; Mousepad `--disable-server`;
    FeatherPad `--standalone`;
  - monotonic start immediately before spawn;
  - discover exact-PID accessible Text + EditableText in editable/focused visible state;
  - inject one short unique ASCII token via AT-SPI keyboard synthesis;
  - stop only after the same accessible reports token acceptance using character-count/caret-local
    evidence;
  - no clipboard, no Save, no user configuration, no window from another PID;
  - dispose the process and isolated state after each run.

One priming run plus >=7 measured runs; report median and p90. Missing AT-SPI/comparator capability is
COMPARATIVE_BLOCKED, never a Graphium product FAIL and never grounds oracle weakening.

SOURCE-ONLY IMPLEMENTATION SLICES
---------------------------------
S1 — Representation state foundation.
S2 — Document menu + status/properties/help synchronization.
S3 — Minimal install projection + packaging/release authority.
S4 — Common FIRST_EDITABLE AT-SPI qualification harness.
S5 — Final source-only convergence / six-menu / anti-bloat / dead-code / canonical audit.

T480 remains forbidden until S1-S5 have exhausted source-provable work and the exact source-only
pre-candidate tree has no blocker. At that point at most one consolidated automated NON-CANDIDATE
platform qualification may be requested if real GTK/AT-SPI/comparator behavior remains indispensable.
Manual tests default to 0.

ENTRY — G12-S1 REPRESENTATION STATE FOUNDATION SOURCE-ONLY CLOSURE
Date: 2026-08-24
State: BINDING / OPEN / NON-CANDIDATE / S1 COMPLETE / S2 NOT AUTHORIZED
================================================================================

User explicitly authorized G12-S1 after the G12 contract freeze. Implementation was performed
source-first with no T480 execution, no manual tests, no Candidate declaration and no Git mutation.

S1 OWNERSHIP
------------
`DocumentSession` remains the single active document-state authority and now owns:
  current_representation_profile;
  saved_representation_profile.

Saved/Modified is exactly the frozen composite relation:
  stable current text state == saved text state
  AND current representation profile == saved representation profile.
There is no second modified boolean and no representation-specific writer.

Open establishes both profiles from the accepted load; New establishes UTF-8/no-BOM/LF.
Explicit encoding selection changes only the current profile and preserves mixed-EOL status.
Explicit LF/CRLF/CR selection changes only the current profile and clears mixed_source because that
choice itself is normalization consent. Unsupported legacy/locale codecs, BOM-less UTF-16/32 and
selectable Mixed EOL remain forbidden.

SAVE / COPY SEMANTICS
---------------------
DocumentSaveService now captures `snapshot.current_representation_profile` in the immutable save
intent rather than reconstructing representation from the accepted file baseline at execution time.
After commit, only the exact captured text state and representation profile advance the saved relation.
A newer representation choice made while I/O is in flight remains Modified. A fresh post-commit load
remains the accepted physical fact; where bytes contain no line separator, its observed/default profile
rebases the unchanged current choice rather than retaining fictitious EOL evidence.

Save a Copy / Save Version Copy serializes the current profile but deliberately does not advance the
active saved relation or rebind the document. GuardedFileWriter remains the sole physical writer.

PERMANENT S1 COVERAGE
---------------------
Added permanent coverage proves:
  representation profile participates in Saved/Modified;
  choosing the saved profile again naturally returns to Saved when text is at its savepoint;
  text Undo does not discard a pending representation choice;
  encoding change alone does not clear mixed_source;
  concrete EOL selection clears mixed_source as explicit normalization consent;
  unsupported encoding/BOM and Mixed/NONE output targets fail closed;
  explicit UTF-16 + CRLF conversion writes exact bytes and becomes clean after accepted commit;
  mixed input with explicit EOL choice needs no second normalization consent;
  late older-profile Save cannot clean a newer profile choice;
  post-commit observed disk representation wins where no physical separator exists;
  non-binding copy uses the current representation and leaves the saved profile untouched.

the same current representation profile. Mixed EOL remains an observation until the user explicitly
chooses LF/CRLF/CR. BOM is shown explicitly for every BOM-bearing profile, including UTF-16/UTF-32.

INSTALL AUTHORITY
-----------------

  default prefix = ~/.local;

The public launcher is a relative symlink so the same staged tree remains valid after package-root
relocation. The private launcher is the existing Graphium launcher byte-for-byte; its existing
repo-relative resource rule also resolves correctly inside `PREFIX/lib/graphium`. The installer
explicitly excludes Python bytecode/cache artifacts when copying the runtime package.

ENTRY — G12-S4 COMMON EXTERNAL FIRST_EDITABLE ORACLE SOURCE-ONLY CLOSURE
Date: 2026-08-24
State: BINDING / OPEN / NON-CANDIDATE / S4 COMPLETE / S5 NOT AUTHORIZED
================================================================================

User explicitly authorized G12-S4 after S3 completed. Work remained source-only: no T480 execution,
manual test, Candidate declaration or Git mutation.

PURPOSE / AUTHORITY BOUNDARY
----------------------------
S4 implements the qualification-only common external FIRST_EDITABLE oracle frozen by G12. It does
not change Graphium runtime, startup, UI, writer, session, monitor, command surface or installation.
No Graphium runtime module imports AT-SPI. The oracle lives only under the permanent desktop
qualification harness and imports GI/Atspi lazily when a real measurement is requested.

COMMON PROTOCOL IMPLEMENTED
---------------------------
Applications:
  graphium
  leafpad
  l3afpad
  mousepad --disable-server
  featherpad --standalone

Workloads:
  empty = named 0-byte fixture
  5KiB  = 5,120-byte fixture
  1MiB  = 1,048,576-byte fixture
  10MiB = 10,485,760-byte fixture

Each run:
  1. creates a disposable HOME plus XDG_CONFIG/DATA/CACHE/STATE roots;
  2. creates the named fixture inside that disposable root;
  3. resolves and spawns the exact comparator process;
  4. starts the monotonic clock immediately before subprocess spawn;
  5. discovers only the AT-SPI application root whose reported process ID equals the spawned PID;
  6. searches only that application's descendants for Text + EditableText with
     EDITABLE + FOCUSED + VISIBLE + SHOWING state;
  7. requires no active text selection;
  8. injects one short unique ASCII token through AT-SPI KEY_STRING keyboard synthesis;
  9. accepts readiness only when the same Text interface reports exact character-count growth,
     caret advance and the token in a small caret-local text range;
 10. terminates the disposable process and discards the isolated environment.

The oracle never uses the clipboard, never invokes Save, never writes user configuration, never
accepts an accessible from a different PID and never relaxes process isolation to make a comparator
pass. A missing executable, unavailable AT-SPI boundary, early process exit or failure to expose an
exact-PID editable text object produces comparative BLOCKED evidence, not Graphium PRODUCT_FAIL.

Measurement series:
  one priming run;
  >=7 measured runs;
  median and p90 reported.

CURRENT AT-SPI API VERIFICATION
-------------------------------
Current AT-SPI 2.0 documentation was checked during S4 source design. It confirms:
  Accessible.get_process_id() returns the process id associated with an accessible;
  Accessible exposes Text and EditableText interface accessors;
  StateSet.contains() owns exact state membership;
  Text exposes character-count, caret-offset and bounded get_text operations;
  generate_keyboard_event() supports composed string input via KEY_STRING.
These facts support the frozen exact-PID/disposable-input protocol. Web/API verification does not
replace the later real T480 platform boundary.

MATURE-SOURCE DISPOSITION
-------------------------
Direct supplied FeatherPad source re-confirms that `--standalone` must be the first option and owns a
separate process. Direct supplied Leafpad/L3afpad source re-confirms ordinary filename invocation.
The previously completed direct Mousepad audit remains authoritative for `--disable-server`; S4 does
not reopen that mature-source decision without contradictory evidence.

ADOPT:
  exact spawned-process ownership;
  disposable local fixture and configuration roots;
  real externally accepted input as the common readiness event;
  one common metric definition for all five applications.
ADAPT:
  AT-SPI Text/EditableText/state/process APIs to Graphium's qualification harness;
  comparator-specific process-isolation switches without relaxing exact-PID ownership.
REJECT:
  FIRST_VISIBLE relabelled as FIRST_EDITABLE;
  clipboard round-trip;
  Save as a readiness side effect;
  xdotool or application-specific scripted editing;
  accepting another process's accessible;
  user HOME/XDG mutation;
  generic benchmark framework;
  AT-SPI runtime dependency in Graphium.
DEFER:
  actual comparative numbers and platform capability verdict until the single consolidated T480
  qualification permitted only after S5 source-only convergence.

STRUCTURAL CONSOLIDATION
------------------------
The first S4 prototype exceeded the permanent validation-LOC ceiling and was rejected before freeze.
No structural gate was rebaselined. The accepted implementation instead:
  removes the unused `tests/desktop/harness/measurements.py` module;
  removes unused runtime descendants/run_owned helpers;
  removes unused fixture write_text/visible_verdict helpers;
  consolidates repeated drain_for/wait_until ownership in the common desktop runtime harness;
  adds S4 protocol coverage to the existing Release entrypoint authority rather than creating a
  separate permanent authority universe.

FINAL COMMAND / MENU AUTHORITY
------------------------------
The six-menu order is now owned once by `graphium.application.commands.TOP_LEVEL_MENUS`:
  File / Edit / Search / View / Document / Help.
The GTK window consumes that authority directly. The previous duplicate hardcoded six-name tuple in
`window.py` is removed. No Tools/Window/Format/Language/Session/Plugins top-level menu exists.

Help converges to exactly:
  User Guide
  Keyboard Shortcuts
  About
About now includes compact support facts for the running Python, GTK and display backend. This closes
the frozen System Information requirement without adding a diagnostics command, page or subsystem.
Keyboard-shortcut documentation was checked against every command-owned accelerator and no mismatch
was found. User Guide documents the same Help surface and the G12 common FIRST_EDITABLE distinction.

Preferences remains exactly two settings:
  Tab width
  Insert spaces instead of tabs
Persistent Status Bar / Line Numbers / Word Wrap / Appearance / Font remain direct View ownership and
are not duplicated in Preferences. Check Now remains inside Properties and is not a command-catalog
entry.

DEAD-CODE / ANTI-BLOAT AUDIT
----------------------------
Static reachability across runtime + permanent tests identified the following definition-only source
with no framework contract and no document/test/runtime reference, and S5 removes it:
  CompositionDescriptor + describe_composition;
  NativeEditorCheckpoint;
  NativeEditorController.abort_native_group wrapper;
  DocumentSession.requires_save_confirmation alias;
  GraphiumPrintController.page_setup copy property;
  GraphiumWindow._string_action_value helper.
After removal, the only definition-only runtime methods are GraphiumApplication.do_activate and
GraphiumApplication.do_open. They are GTK/GApplication virtual overrides and are explicitly retained.
No writer, identity authority, savepoint state, monitor ownership or lifecycle safety code is removed.

MATURE-SOURCE CHECK ON ABOUT SCOPE
----------------------------------
Direct supplied Leafpad and FeatherPad About implementations confirm a compact application-information
surface rather than a new top-level diagnostics workflow. Gedit/GTK-family Help patterns likewise keep
About under Help. Graphium ADAPTs this narrowly by adding only support-relevant runtime facts already
available inside the GTK adapter. A diagnostics platform, log collector or new menu command is REJECTED.

ENTRY — G12 FIRST CONSOLIDATED PLATFORM STOP / DEEP MATURE+CALAMUS ORACLE REBUILD
Date: 2026-08-25

PLATFORM STOP PRESERVED
-----------------------
The first authorized consolidated G12 NON-CANDIDATE T480 qualification executed the exact S5 tree.
Lanes 1-14 passed, including 154 Behavioral, 126 Integration, 13 Packaging/Release, Structural
Continuity and all 10 permanent True-GTK sections. Lane 15 (`G12 GTK CLOSURE`) then failed before any
Lane-15 semantic checkpoint was emitted. Lanes 16-18 were not reached. No Candidate was declared or
consumed and no Git mutation occurred.

Failure-specific source-first comparison was then expanded beyond the immediate Lane-15 stop to direct
mature-source evidence from Leafpad, L3afpad, Mousepad 0.7.0, FeatherPad, GNOME Text Editor and gedit,
plus the preserved Calamus W116 completion-oracle lesson. The immediate failure was source-proven as a
qualification ownership defect: the new helper queried `Gtk.Application.get_menubar()` although
Graphium intentionally creates and owns the concrete visible `Gtk.MenuBar` inside `GraphiumWindow`.
No product menu-owner failure was established.

BROADER ORACLE CLASS DEFECT
---------------------------
The deeper audit proved that the initial S4 completion rule was also invalid before lanes 16-18 were
ever executed. Leafpad, L3afpad and FeatherPad can expose their editor before requested command-line
Open completes; Graphium's own `do_open()` also presents the window before `open_path()` finishes.
Therefore focused/visible `EditableText` plus accepted synthetic input does not prove that the named
5 KiB / 1 MiB / 10 MiB workload has loaded. The original S4 `empty` case had also drifted into a named
0-byte file rather than true no-file startup.

Additional source-proven qualification defects found before another T480 run:
  - comparator BLOCKED could be misreported as overall PASS;
  - FIRST_VISIBLE timestamping was retrodated and X11 probing polluted the FIRST_EDITABLE loop;
  - G07 input-contamination ownership was missing from the new AT-SPI series;
  - RSS sampled at readiness/fixed delay was incorrectly called idle RSS;
  - priming/session-first, raw runs and workload hashes were not retained in a complete receipt;
  - comparator execution order was not balanced;
  - installed-product platform smoke did not exercise real file Open + installed User Guide action.

The common causal rule is the Calamus W116 / Graphium G04-G07 rule already learned earlier:
  EVENT / ACTION / FOCUS / ELAPSED TIME != SEMANTIC COMPLETION.
No runtime product repair is permitted merely to satisfy such an oracle.

SOURCE-ONLY REBUILD
-------------------
The complete Lane-15..18 qualification design was rebuilt before any retry.

Permanent common FIRST_EDITABLE protocol now owns only reusable evidence primitives:
  - `empty` command has no pathname;
  - non-empty fixtures have exact byte/character size and workload-specific BEGIN/END sentinels;
  - exact-PID AT-SPI Text+EditableText discovery remains required;
  - requested workload count + sentinels must be externally present before synthetic input;
  - exact PID/focus/workload is revalidated immediately before KEY_STRING synthesis;
  - acceptance remains local count/caret/token evidence with no clipboard and no Save;
  - the old platform-running CLI/`one_run()` authority is removed from the permanent module.

Rebuilt external G12 platform authorities are deliberately outside product/runtime and permanent test
universes. Their source-only verifier proves:
  - Lane 15 inspects the concrete window-owned Gtk.MenuBar and emits semantic failure markers;
  - representation transitions use synchronous DocumentSession/action/status postconditions;
  - About and installed User Guide use responders armed before modal-capable activation;
  - FIRST_VISIBLE and FIRST_EDITABLE are separate series;
  - X11 probing is absent from the FIRST_EDITABLE loop;
  - a key/button contamination tripwire invalidates the complete execution before controlled input;
  - comparator BLOCKED => comparative receipt BLOCKED/INCOMPLETE, never PASS;
  - receipt retains versions, workload SHA-256, priming/session-first, raw runs, deterministic balanced
    run order, median/p90, reasons and target verdicts;
  - semantic idle RSS requires a stable five-sample window and is distinct from RSS_AT_FIRST_EDITABLE;
  - installed smoke opens a real disposable 5 KiB file through the installed launcher and exercises
    the real installed User Guide action.

REPRESENTATION MAPPING CONSOLIDATION
------------------------------------
The mature-source audit also exposed a smaller ownership-drift risk: Encoding/EOL choice semantics
were named in the command catalog but independently mapped again in `window.py`. The rebuild keeps
DocumentSession as the only representation-state authority while moving choice value ->
encoding/BOM/line-ending mapping into the existing application command authority. GTK now consumes
that mapping. GuardedFileWriter, DocumentSession Saved/Modified semantics, save service, strong monitor
and filesystem safety are otherwise unchanged. Runtime `graphium/` is net -4 text LOC versus S5.

CANONICAL S4 AMENDMENT
----------------------
Architecture Contract section 22.5 is amended: non-empty common FIRST_EDITABLE requires exact workload
load completion before input; empty is true no-file startup; FIRST_VISIBLE is measured separately;
input contamination invalidates evidence; BLOCKED is incomplete rather than PASS; the receipt retains
all required raw/hash/version/order evidence; RSS_AT_FIRST_EDITABLE and semantic IDLE_RSS are distinct.
Roadmap records the first platform stop and this source-first rebuild. Historical MO entries remain
unchanged under append-only policy.

DEEP MATURE-SOURCE / HISTORICAL CLASSIFICATION
----------------------------------------------
Direct re-audit against Leafpad, L3afpad, Mousepad, FeatherPad, gedit, GNOME Text Editor, historical
Graphium G04/G07 and Calamus W116 proves that the common external FIRST_EDITABLE model itself was
invalid for v1, not merely another implementation bug:

  - G07's mandatory post-exit X11 quiescence rule had been dropped;
  - FIRST_VISIBLE required pre-snapshot XID novelty in addition to exact PID even though exact PID is
    the semantic owner;
  - the generic AT-SPI predicate collapsed accessibility exposure, VISIBLE/SHOWING, EDITABLE, FOCUSED,
    file-load completion and sentinel propagation into one ambiguous timeout;
  - focus is desktop/window-manager state, not document-load completion;
  - AT-SPI Text is an accessibility projection, not a universal file-loader transaction receipt;
  - `EditableText.insert_text()` is direct accessibility mutation, not a universal proof that the
    application's real user-keyboard pipeline is ready;
  - blocked diagnostics regressed below G07's already-frozen command/PID/rc/window/output capture.

Mature products correctly own completion differently: Leafpad/L3afpad have synchronous file-open
completion; gedit and GNOME Text Editor expose explicit loading state; FeatherPad owns asynchronous
loading/completion; Graphium owns `_startup_open_pending` and its exact inherited-pipe READY record.
Calamus W116 already established the general rule that an observable proxy/action is not the lifecycle
completion authority unless the product/toolkit actually owns that postcondition.

BINDING REBASELINE
------------------
The G12 common external FIRST_EDITABLE experiment is RETIRED. It must not be repaired repeatedly until
it happens to pass.

Final v1 performance model:

  1. FIRST_VISIBLE is the sole homogeneous cross-product latency metric for Graphium, Leafpad,
     L3afpad, Mousepad and FeatherPad.
  2. Graphium FIRST_EDITABLE remains exact Graphium-only evidence transported by the inherited READY
     pipe after requested Open completion + mapping + focus. The already valid Lane-17 G12-vs-G11
     self-regression remains the relevant final Graphium editable-readiness evidence.
  3. The previous G12 1.5x/1.75x Mousepad FIRST_EDITABLE targets are WITHDRAWN AS INVALID. They are not
     silently relabelled or transplanted onto FIRST_VISIBLE.
  4. Existing G04 FIRST_VISIBLE hard admissions remain binding: empty <=2.0x Mousepad or <=750 ms;
     5 KiB <=2.0x Mousepad or <=900 ms. 1 MiB/10 MiB comparator gaps are reported without inventing
     new ratio thresholds.
  5. The independent G12 memory target remains: Graphium stable post-visible RSS <=150 MiB and <=2.5x
     Mousepad. This is a process-level stable-memory observation, not a claim of universal internal
     application-idle lifecycle knowledge.

PERMANENT SOURCE REDUCTION
--------------------------
The rejected `tests/desktop/harness/first_editable.py` authority is removed. It is replaced by
`tests/desktop/harness/comparators.py`, which owns only genuinely common comparator primitives:

  - exact app set;
  - isolated command construction;
  - Mousepad `--disable-server`;
  - FeatherPad `--standalone`;
  - true no-file empty workload;
  - exact-size 5 KiB / 1 MiB / 10 MiB fixture bytes;
  - disposable HOME/XDG environment.

There is no AT-SPI, Accessible Text/EditableText, focus, sentinel-as-lifecycle, keyboard synthesis or
direct accessibility mutation in the permanent cross-product protocol.

FIRST_VISIBLE / RSS AUTHORITY
-----------------------------
The rebaselined qualification-only external authority freezes the G04/G07 mature model:

  - start monotonic clock immediately before process spawn;
  - detect the first CURRENT X11 top-level whose `_NET_WM_PID` equals the exact spawned PID;
  - do not require XID novelty relative to a pre-spawn set;
  - after every sample, terminate/kill if needed and wait boundedly until no X11 top-level remains
    owned by the exited PID before starting another fresh sample;
  - non-quiescence, no-window, process forwarding/exit or missing comparator is BLOCKED_INCOMPLETE,
    never Graphium product FAIL and never PASS;
  - blocked evidence preserves command, exact PID, process rc, exact-PID window snapshots and bounded
    stdout/stderr;
  - persist the receipt incrementally after every priming/measured run;
  - retain one priming/session-first observation plus seven measured runs;
  - measured order is deterministic and alternates forward/reverse to reduce drift;
  - any real RawKeyPress/RawButtonPress during the measured interval invalidates the execution; the
    benchmark generates no input itself.

Stable post-visible RSS is measured only for Graphium and Mousepad using true no-file startup. After
exact-PID FIRST_VISIBLE, five consecutive process-RSS samples must span at least 0.4 s and have <=1.0
MiB spread before the median is accepted. A fixed delay is not called idle.

================================================================================
ENTRY — G13 OPENING + S1 RECOVERY ARTIFACT/STORAGE CORE
Date: 2026-08-25
State: BINDING / G13 OPEN / S1 COMPLETE / NON-CANDIDATE
================================================================================

AUTHORIZATION AND BASELINE
--------------------------
The user separately authorized opening G13 after the final post-sync audit/MO-only canonicalization,
then explicitly authorized G13-S1. The canonical parent state for this source-only work is:

  BASE_HEAD=f32beeeca58fdc4d68b7d9253ec98d2b76b38018
  BASE_TREE=23c6dde1b69f36b71dcaa6eb0deb4b19f2370075
  BASE_PRODUCT_SUBTREE=1eb5c018574d330907d7f0cab0353074e7b37fe6
  BASE_WORKTREE=CLEAN

G13 opening completed source-first against Mousepad, GNOME Text Editor, gedit and FeatherPad before
implementation. Frozen central rule: recovery is private restart-persistent state, never Save and never
the accepted user target. Mousepad/GNOME auxiliary/draft separation is ADOPT/ADAPT; gedit/FeatherPad
autosave-to-target is REJECT for G13.

S1 IMPLEMENTED BOUNDARY
-----------------------
S1 adds only the GTK-free storage foundation:
  - pure versioned `RecoveryRecord` codec with strict UTF-8 body, length and SHA-256 self-validation;
  - current/saved representation profile metadata without target serialization;
  - named baseline provenance or identity-free Untitled records;
  - pure `XDG_STATE_HOME/graphium/recovery` resolution;
  - canonical random UUID artifact identity and UUID-derived filenames only;
  - private 0700 root / 0600 artifact+lock files;
  - atomic temp -> fsync -> readback length/digest -> replace -> directory fsync persistence;
  - POSIX advisory ownership lock compatible with NON_UNIQUE Graphium processes;
  - corrupt/truncated/version/digest/UTF-8 mismatch rejection;
  - no GTK, timer, worker, DocumentSession/lifecycle integration or restore UI.

The storage API accepts no arbitrary target destination and imports neither normal Save service nor
`GuardedFileWriter`. `GuardedFileWriter` remains the sole user-document writer authority.

PERMANENT-GATE CLASSIFICATION
-----------------------------
The first full local run exposed one release-oracle mismatch: the historical `single_physical_writer`
gate classified any `os.replace/fsync` outside its old allowlist as a second document writer. The frozen
G13 contract itself requires a distinct private recovery-state writer and forbids using `GuardedFileWriter`
for recovery. Source-first reinspection therefore classified this as authority-gate drift, not product
FAIL. The gate was narrowly adapted only to allow `graphium/infrastructure/recovery_store.py`; its
existing assertion that `GuardedFileWriter` is the only `*Writer` document authority remains unchanged.
Permanent S1 tests separately prove the recovery API has no arbitrary destination path.

PRODUCT-LINE DECISION
---------------------
The user has additionally frozen the cumulative edition roadmap:
  Graphium Core = current lightweight editor + G13 Recovery + G14 External Spellcheck.
  Graphium Plus = Core + compact icon toolbar + Writing Workspace adapted from Calamus.
  Graphium Ultra = Plus + Markdown viewer/editor.
G14 belongs to Core and must be optional/on-demand external spellcheck; Plus/Ultra are NOT OPENED.

--------------------------

Permanent full qualification after the gate adaptation is required to remain PASS before delivery.

ENTRY — G13-S2 RECOVERY STATE-MACHINE / LIFECYCLE INTEGRATION
Date: 2026-08-25
State: BINDING / G13 OPEN / S1-S2 COMPLETE / NON-CANDIDATE
================================================================================

AUTHORIZATION / SCOPE
---------------------
The user explicitly authorized the next step after G13-S1. S2 remains NON-CANDIDATE and source-first:
no Git mutation, T480 run, manual test, Candidate declaration or publication is authorized here.

S2 IMPLEMENTED BOUNDARY
-----------------------
S2 connects the S1 private artifact to the live single-document state machine without adding recovery
presentation UI. The accepted design is:
  - fixed RECOVERY_DELAY_SECONDS=30, one-shot and non-resetting while pending;
  - native buffer full capture only at that timed boundary, on the GTK/main context;
  - repeated edits while a timer is pending collapse into that one newest capture;
  - exactly one dedicated recovery worker, created lazily as an executor thread only when work is
    submitted; no generic job framework or permanent background scan;
  - while a recovery write is in flight, later changes collapse into one newest-pending state and at
    most one subsequent timer;
  - storage now separates durable staging from final atomic publication so controller generation
    invalidation can fence stale worker completion before os.replace;
  - publication/invalidation share a narrow lock only around generation check + final replace/fsync;
  - accepted invalidation wins over stale completion; if publication wins first, cleanup follows;
  - clean process close drains the dedicated recovery queue so queued stale-write discard/removal is
    not cancelled and cannot become a false crash orphan;
  - NativeEditorController emits a narrow document-state notification after successful committed
    editor state changes, Undo/Redo and successful New/Open installation, never during failed rollback;
  - FileLifecycleController notifies recovery only after successful Save/Save As and invalidates only
    after accepted close; Cancel/failed Save/Open/Reload preserve current recovery;
  - representation-only Encoding/EOL changes explicitly notify the recovery state machine;
  - Save a Copy / Version Copy remains nonbinding and has no recovery invalidation path;
  - recovery success/failure never advances Saved state, changes DocumentFileState, or writes the user
    target.

STRUCTURAL CONSOLIDATION
------------------------
The first straightforward S2 hostile-test addition exceeded the unrebaselined permanent validation-LOC
ceiling. The ceiling was NOT weakened or rebaselined. Existing duplicated native-buffer test doubles in
Behavioral/Integration tests were consolidated into one concern-owned shared test support module. S2
coverage was then kept compact. This is a real authority/duplication reduction, not whitespace-only or
cosmetic gate gaming.

USER-PC COMMAND DELIVERY POLICY — BINDING CLARIFICATION
-------------------------------------------------------
The MO already required copy/paste-ready terminal instructions when indispensable human/T480 validation
is requested. The user explicitly asked that this be stated for every user-PC test request, not only
human-manual validation. Binding clarification from this entry onward:

  EVERY_USER_PC_TEST_REQUEST_INCLUDES_TERMINAL_COMMAND_SAME_MESSAGE=YES
  TERMINAL_COMMAND_MUST_BE_COMPLETE_COPY_PASTE_READY=YES
  TEST_PACKAGE_AND_COMMAND_MUST_BE_DELIVERED_TOGETHER=YES
  NO_USER_PC_TEST_REQUEST_WITHOUT_ITS_EXACT_LAUNCH_COMMAND=YES

This supplements, and does not replace, the existing last-resort T480/source-first/manual-test policy.
If no user-PC execution is required, no terminal command is invented merely for ceremony.

QUALIFICATION / ACCOUNTING
--------------------------

coverage for lazy clean startup, exact 30-second coalescing, timed full capture, Untitled/Unicode,
representation-only recovery, Undo-to-savepoint invalidation, stale worker fencing, one-pending-after-
in-flight behavior, clean-close fencing, failed Open preservation, successful replacement timing and
Close Cancel/Discard semantics. Full permanent qualification and Structural Continuity must PASS from
the exact delivered bytes before S2 can be called complete.

ENTRY — G13-S3 STARTUP RECOVERY DISCOVERY / PRESENTATION / RESTORE
Date: 2026-08-25
State: BINDING / G13 OPEN / S1-S3 COMPLETE / NON-CANDIDATE
================================================================================

AUTHORIZATION / SCOPE
---------------------
The user explicitly authorized the next step after G13-S2. S3 remains NON-CANDIDATE and source-first:
no Git mutation, T480 run, manual test, Candidate declaration or publication is authorized here.

S3 IMPLEMENTED BOUNDARY
-----------------------
S3 makes the frozen recovery cache visible only at process startup, without introducing a session
manager or general restore platform:
  - discovery is lazy and does not create XDG recovery storage when the directory is absent;
  - published UUID artifacts are validated before candidacy; corrupt/malformed entries are never offered;
  - valid artifacts are ordered newest-first by capture time; one process offers at most one;
  - a candidate is claimed with its advisory lock and reread under that lock before presentation, so two
    NON_UNIQUE Graphium processes cannot validly offer the same orphan concurrently;
  - plain activation may offer the newest valid unlocked orphan; explicit file-open startup may offer only
    a named artifact associated with that normalized logical path, never an unrelated orphan;
  - startup choice is exactly Recover Unsaved Content / Discard Recovery / Start Without Recovering;
  - Start Without Recovering releases ownership and retains the artifact; Discard removes only the selected
    artifact explicitly; other valid artifacts remain untouched for later launches;
  - Recover performs the existing strong document load/observation before any named binding is adopted;
  - named baseline match requires the same normalized logical path, canonical target, device/inode when
    known, and accepted SHA-256 content fingerprint;
  - exact match establishes the freshly loaded disk object as Saved baseline and installs recovered text as
    a fresh Modified state with recovered current Encoding/BOM/EOL profile and empty Undo/Redo;
  - missing/content-replaced/object-replaced/symlink-retargeted targets recover fail-closed as unbound
    Modified content; the old path is UI provenance only and later writing therefore requires normal Save As;
  - Untitled recovery is always unbound Modified and invents no file identity;
  - the claimed orphan artifact remains owned after successful Recover and is reused until later accepted
    Save/Discard/lifecycle invalidation; recovery restoration itself never gains Save authority;
  - the dedicated recovery ThreadPoolExecutor is now fully lazy and is instantiated only on first submitted
    persistence work, so a no-recovery clean startup starts no recovery executor/thread.

VISIBLE UX / USER GUIDE
-----------------------
The GTK adapter adds one narrow startup MessageDialog titled `Recovered unsaved content`. It has no menu,
preferences, history browser, tab/session surface or background recovery status UI. The offline User Guide
is updated in this same S3 tree because recovery is now user-visible.

STRUCTURAL CONTINUITY
---------------------
The first complete S3 hostile-test addition raised permanent validation LOC above the unrebaselined ceiling.
The ceiling was NOT raised. Existing lifecycle-test setup/preservation duplication was consolidated into
shared helpers and a smaller test UI, retaining all previous assertions plus the new S3 cases. Final
Structural Continuity remains within the existing budget.

HOSTILE COVERAGE ADDED
----------------------
S3 permanent tests cover: absent-root lazy startup; newest-orphan selection; Start Without retention;
explicit-path filtering; live-lock exclusion; explicit discard isolation; corrupt-artifact exclusion; exact
named baseline recovery; same-bytes inode replacement -> unbound; symlink retarget -> unbound; missing
target -> unbound; Untitled unbound restore; recovered representation state; Modified state with empty
Undo/Redo; fresh Saved baseline identity for exact named recovery; and lazy recovery executor creation.

-------------------------------

on the PC/T480 must deliver the exact package and the complete copy/paste-ready terminal command in the
same message. S3 requires no user-PC execution.

ENTRY — G14 OPENING / S1 EXTERNAL PROTOCOL + UNICODE SPAN CORE
Date: 2026-08-25
===============================================================================

AUTHORIZATION / BASELINE
------------------------
The user explicitly authorized G14 after FINAL_POST_G13_SYNC_READONLY_AUDIT=PASS, then explicitly authorized the next serial step G14-S1. Canonical parent remains post-G13-sync commit `8a847a793b9d84f76161c41cce261dd82b3deb17`, tree `65318ce6847304ccbcce31767311857fb42798f3`; G13 product publication remains `053bcde3f5bcb4f51ce9edd8a89538a7630949ae` / `eb6925d3b779fa8ae12d1d0947a31fe460fbee0e`.

G14 opening completed source-first / mature-source / Hunspell-protocol audit and froze External Spellcheck as a Core capability. G14-S1 is NON-CANDIDATE only. No Git mutation, Candidate declaration, Candidate attempt, T480 or manual test is authorized or consumed.

S1 IMPLEMENTED BOUNDARY
-----------------------
Product additions are confined to:
  graphium/domain/spellcheck.py
  graphium/infrastructure/hunspell_session.py

The domain module owns immutable WordSpan plus a lazy bounded Unicode code-point span iterator. Letters/combining marks are lexical material; internal ASCII apostrophe, U+2019 apostrophe and simple hyphen are preserved only inside lexical spans; spans without alphabetic code points and pathological overlong spans are skipped without normalizing/case-folding the document.

The infrastructure module owns only the optional Hunspell child boundary. Resolver work happens only when explicitly called. Session executable identity is an absolute resolved path. Spawn argv is `hunspell -a -i UTF-8 --check-apostrophe`, `shell=False`; the document pathname is not an API input and is never sent. Every token line is prefixed with `^`. Input token bytes, output lines, suggestion counts and suggestion sizes are bounded. Protocol UTF-8 and response grammar are strict. Timeout, cancellation, child/protocol fault and Close terminate/reap the child; one request stream is serialized per session. No process/thread/I/O is wired into startup by S1.

SOURCE / TEST AUTHORITY
-----------------------
A permanent release gate confines product `subprocess.Popen` ownership to the existing NON_UNIQUE launcher and this Hunspell session boundary, and asserts the frozen Hunspell argv/data/shell/GTK restrictions. Fake-Hunspell subprocess tests exercise real pipes without requiring Hunspell installation. Existing integration test modules were structurally compacted only by deleting blank lines; executable statements/assertions were not removed. Structural Continuity is not rebaselined.

ENTRY — G14-S2 SPELL SESSION + GRAPHium EDIT AUTHORITY
Date: 2026-08-25
State: BINDING / OPEN / S1+S2 COMPLETE / NON-CANDIDATE
================================================================================

AUTHORIZATION / SERIAL BOUNDARY
-------------------------------
The user explicitly authorized G14-S2 after G14-S1 PASS. This authorization covered only the
GTK-free spell-session/edit-authority slice. It did not authorize G14-S3 GTK projection, Candidate,
Candidate attempt consumption, Git mutation, T480/manual validation, Graphium Plus or Graphium Ultra.

S2 IMPLEMENTED AUTHORITY
------------------------
`graphium/application/spellcheck.py` owns one dialog-lifetime spell session state machine. It captures
an exact editor text/state-id snapshot through `NativeEditorController.capture_programmatic_source()`;
scans from a bounded code-point cursor; emits immutable `SpellCheckRequest` values for external checking;
accepts only the exact pending result; projects one current `SpellIssue`; keeps Ignore-All as an exact-token
session-only set; and closes/stales fail-closed. External Hunspell I/O remains outside this controller so S3
can dispatch the existing S1 pipe request off the GTK main thread and return only the result.

Replacement planning is GTK-free. `plan_spell_replacement()` validates the exact source span, produces
bounded delete/insert ReplayOperations, prevalidates final renderability and collapses the target view at
the end of the explicit accepted replacement. `SpellCheckController.replace()` re-captures current text/view,
requires exact source state identity, then delegates the final mutation exclusively to
`NativeEditorController.apply_prevalidated_programmatic_group()`. No alternate Gtk.TextBuffer writer, Save
writer or filesystem path is introduced. Each changed Replace is one ordinary Undo/Redo group; identical
replacement is an exact no-op; deletion/custom replacement are normal explicit edits; Encoding/BOM/EOL
profile remains untouched; disk remains unchanged until ordinary Save.

`NativeEditorController` now exposes only the minimal generic programmatic-source capture/state-id surface
needed to fence this and other explicit programmatic edit planners. It does not acquire spell semantics.

COMMAND IDENTITY
----------------
The single command authority now freezes `CHECK_SPELLING_COMMAND` as:
  action=check-spelling
  label=Check Spelling…
  accelerator=F2
  menu=Document
It is deliberately NOT yet included in `COMMANDS`; therefore S2 does not project a menu item, accelerator
or GTK action prematurely. S3 must add the same CommandSpec object to the existing projection path rather
than create a parallel GTK command authority.

ENTRY — G14-S3 THIN GTK SPELLCHECK PROJECTION

AUTHORIZATION / SCOPE
---------------------
The user explicitly authorized G14-S3 after S2 PASS. S3 is limited to the thin GTK projection of the
already frozen S1/S2 spellcheck authorities: Document -> Check Spelling… / F2, a minimal modal dialog,
off-main-thread Hunspell request execution, worker -> GLib main-context result delivery, help text and
user-visible capability/failure messages. Candidate declaration, Git mutation and T480/manual validation
are not authorized by this slice.

S3 IMPLEMENTED BOUNDARY
-----------------------
`CHECK_SPELLING_COMMAND` is now projected exactly once through the product-owned command catalog under
Document with F2. `GraphiumWindow` lazy-imports the GTK spelling adapter only when that action is activated;
normal application startup and composition do not import the spelling adapter, resolve Hunspell, create a
spell executor/thread, spawn a child or scan dictionaries.

`graphium/adapters/gtk/spelling.py` is a thin projection over `SpellCheckController` and
`HunspellPipeSession`. One explicit dialog owns one controller and one Hunspell session. A single-worker
executor is created only when the first actual token request must be sent. Hunspell I/O stays off the GTK
main thread; completion returns through `GLib.idle_add`. Close/fault cancels the session, terminates/reaps
the child through the S1 authority and shuts down the worker. The GTK adapter does not receive or send the
document pathname and does not create another editor/save/filesystem mutation authority.

The UI exposes only System default dictionary, Unknown word, bounded suggestions/custom replacement,
Replace, Ignore, Ignore All and Close. Missing Hunspell or process/dictionary/protocol failure is reported
without document mutation. Every accepted Replace remains the existing S2 normal Graphium edit/Undo unit.
User Guide and Keyboard Shortcuts document the on-demand semantics and F2. No live underlining, background
spell service, language detection, persistent language preference, Add to Dictionary, Correct All,
autocorrect or grammar checking is added.

G15-S3 HUNSPELL DICTIONARY SELECTION — NON-CANDIDATE IMPLEMENTATION
------------------------------------------------------------------
The mandatory source-first audit compared Mousepad 0.7.0, gedit, GNOME Text Editor, FeatherPad, Leafpad,
L3afpad and direct Hunspell CLI behavior before implementation. The frozen design keeps the existing lazy
external Hunspell boundary and converts the dialog's static Dictionary row into a real combo: System default
plus verified installed Hunspell dictionary IDs. No GUI-language selector is introduced.

Discovery occurs only after explicit Check Spelling invocation, through the existing single spell worker, by
running bounded/cancellable `hunspell -D` with `LC_ALL=C`. Human headings are ignored; only absolute base
paths backed by current `.aff` + `.dic` regular-file pairs are selectable. Output/path/count budgets are
enforced and duplicate IDs are disambiguated. System default preserves the existing pipe argv; explicit
selection adds exactly one `-d <base>` pair and the pair is revalidated at child start.

Dictionary selection is dialog-local and non-persistent. Switching dictionary fences old callbacks, reaps the
old Hunspell child, rejects externally stale editor state, snapshots current Graphium text with a fresh spell
controller, resets Ignore All and restarts from the beginning. Previous Replace edits remain ordinary Undo/Redo
edits. No config/schema, document metadata, new module, dependency, framework, daemon or startup probe exists.

Focused boundary tests passed and local permanent qualification passed Behavioral 190/190, Integration
176/176 and Packaging/Release 27/27 = 393/393. The focused T480 True-GTK run subsequently passed with real
Hunspell discovery `en_US,it_CH,it_IT`, real GTK combo enumeration, explicit dictionary switch/restart and
Ignore All reset, real child cleanup, document neutrality and external-edit stale fencing. Source/package
identity remained unchanged. Manual tests=0; attempt consumed=0; Git mutation=0.

G15-S4 TRANSFORM TEXT SHORTCUTS — NON-CANDIDATE IMPLEMENTATION
--------------------------------------------------------------
Mandatory source-first comparison covered Mousepad, FeatherPad, gedit and additional mature Linux editor
shortcut conventions. FeatherPad's symmetric Ctrl+Shift+U/L pair was not copied because GTK3 owns
Ctrl+Shift+U for Unicode code-point input. Ctrl+Alt+L was rejected because Cinnamon owns it for lock screen;
Ctrl+Alt letter bindings and chord infrastructure were also rejected as unnecessary complexity.

A read-only T480 namespace audit on Cinnamon 6.6.9 / GTK 3.24.41 / X11 / Italian XKB proved the final pair:
Ctrl+U = Uppercase; Ctrl+Shift+L = Lowercase. Both were valid GTK accelerators with no Graphium namespace,
Gtk.TextView, GTK Unicode-input or active Cinnamon global collision.

Implementation changes only the existing CommandSpec accelerator fields plus the two Help authorities and
permanent tests. Existing text-transform code, selection-only enablement, transaction/history semantics and
document safety are unchanged. No new product module, dependency, setting, persistence, keyboard manager,
thread or subprocess is introduced. Ctrl+Shift+U and Ctrl+Alt+L are explicit negative oracles.

ENTRY — G16 / ABOUT ICON CORRECTIVE CLOSURE — NON-CANDIDATE LOCAL PASS / TRUE-GTK PENDING
Date: 2026-08-26
State: BINDING / POST-G15 CORRECTIVE LINE / NO CANDIDATE
================================================================================

TRIGGER AND CLASSIFICATION
--------------------------
After G15 0.0.15 was published and canonically converged, a real user screenshot of Help > About showed the
GTK missing-image placeholder instead of the Graphium logo. This is a genuine post-G15 product/oracle gap and
does not rewrite G15 certification history. The G15-S5 oracle had accepted a non-null 16x16 pixbuf without
proving Graphium-logo identity and therefore produced a false positive for the About logo property.

MANDATORY SOURCE-FIRST RE-AUDIT
--------------------------------
Direct comparison with Mousepad 0.7.0, Leafpad, L3afpad, GNOME Text Editor and FeatherPad confirms that mature
editors explicitly project their application icon into About. GTK3's GtkAboutDialog `logo-icon-name` defaults
to `image-missing`; leaving both About logo properties unset therefore does not safely inherit Graphium's
default-window icon identity.

G16 NON-CANDIDATE IMPLEMENTATION
--------------------------------
The correction remains inside existing owner `graphium/adapters/gtk/dialogs.py`. About first asks the current
GTK icon theme whether the canonical `APPLICATION_ICON_NAME` (`io.github.leviagravia.Graphium`) is available;
when available it explicitly sets that same icon-name. If the named theme icon is unavailable during direct
source execution, About reuses the already-loaded default Graphium window icon list and selects the exact
hand-tuned 48x48 pixbuf. No SVG path, About-specific asset, second icon authority, GResource, module,
dependency, configuration key, thread or subprocess is added. Version remains 0.0.15 during NON-CANDIDATE
implementation.

PERMANENT ORACLE REPAIR
-----------------------
The former S5 no-explicit-logo assertion is superseded. Permanent release tests now require explicit projection
of the existing application-icon authority, the exact 48x48 source fallback, unchanged bounded five-file hicolor
asset topology and absence of an About-specific resource/path. Future True-GTK proof must compare actual logo
identity and reject the previous image-missing/16x16 false-positive class.

LOCAL QUALIFICATION
-------------------
Behavioral: 190/190 PASS
Integration: 176/176 PASS
Packaging/Release: 45/45 PASS
Total: 411/411 PASS
Focused G16 release gates: 5/5 PASS
Manual tests: 0 planned if exact True-GTK identity can be proven automatically.

CURRENT STATE
-------------

ENTRY — G16 / ABOUT TRUE-GTK PASS + TWO PRE-CANDIDATE BLOCKERS LOCAL PASS
Date: 2026-08-26
State: BINDING / NON-CANDIDATE / TRUE-GTK BLOCKER PROBE PENDING
================================================================================

The focused G16 About-icon T480 probe passed exact Graphium identity in both isolated source-run and staged-installed modes, rejecting image-missing. Its one Gtk-CRITICAL was harness-only: set_custom_theme() was incorrectly called on a screen-singleton IconTheme; product assertions and source/package identity all passed.

Before Candidate-readiness, two user-visible defects were reported and source-first audited: explicit Light appearance left the custom Gtk.TextView LEFT border-window gutter black, and real installed Hunspell with it_IT failed on a legitimate multi-record `hunspell -a` response group while presenting misleading installation guidance. Candidate-readiness is therefore blocked until these are platform-proven.

The frozen RED->GREEN implementation preserves all existing architecture. The gutter now paints its native LEFT border-window background from the same GtkStyleContext used by the editor before drawing line-number glyphs. Hunspell now reads one bounded response group until the documented blank terminator (64 records / 64 KiB / one group timeout), preserves single-record suggestions, aggregates multi-record all-correct as correct, and conservatively returns no whole-span suggestions when any component is misspelled. Protocol, timeout and runtime-process messages no longer claim Hunspell is absent after discovery has succeeded. No tokenizer, dependency, module, config, thread or new subprocess type is added.

Local qualification: 190/190 Behavioral + 180/180 Integration + 50/50 Packaging/Release = 421/421 PASS. Runtime modules remain 57 and runtime delta versus the About-only G16 source is +65 LOC. Version remains 0.0.15, Candidate NO, attempt NO, Git mutation NO. Next step is one focused automated T480 probe covering Light/Dark/System gutter pixels and real installed it_IT multi-record Hunspell synchronization; manual tests target 0.

----- BEGIN VERBATIM GITHUB README.md -----
<p align="center">
  <img src="assets/graphium.svg" width="128" height="128" alt="Graphium logo">
</p>

<h1 align="center">Graphium</h1>

<p align="center">
  <strong>Fast · Simple · Safe · Native GTK</strong>
</p>

<p align="center">
  A lightweight plain-text editor for Linux with unusually strict file-safety semantics.
</p>

Graphium is a native **GTK 3 / Gtk.TextView** editor for quick single-file work.

It deliberately keeps the classic lightweight-editor model — **one process, one window, one active document** — while adding careful file handling, exact Saved/Modified state, representation preservation, recovery, printing, spell checking and the everyday conveniences expected from a mature desktop editor.

Graphium has no projects, plugin platform, cloud service, hidden document database, syntax-highlighting subsystem or multi-document tab model.

## Why Graphium?

### Lightweight by design

Graphium is continuously benchmarked against **Leafpad, L3afpad, Mousepad and FeatherPad**.

Certified comparative runs place Graphium in the **same practical lightweight performance class** as these editors: startup and working-set results are broadly comparable for ordinary quick-edit use, although different editors lead in different workloads.

Graphium is not designed to win every microbenchmark. Its goal is to remain lightweight while providing stronger guarantees around the file being edited.

### Where Graphium goes further

**Compared with Leafpad and L3afpad**, Graphium keeps a similarly direct single-document workflow but provides a substantially broader trust and desktop-completeness layer: guarded saving, external-file monitoring, recovery, explicit encoding and line-ending control, printing, spell checking, appearance controls and richer file-state information.

**Compared with Mousepad**, Graphium deliberately keeps a smaller architectural surface: no server-style document sharing, no tabs, no syntax/language subsystem and no plugin platform. At the same time, its Save model explicitly separates editor state from filesystem state and treats external notifications only as triggers for fresh observation.

**Compared with FeatherPad**, Graphium is intentionally narrower. FeatherPad is stronger for users who need tabs, sessions, syntax highlighting or power-editor features; Graphium instead focuses on a single-document GTK workflow with a smaller conceptual surface and stricter file-preservation rules.

Graphium's clearest advantage is therefore not feature count alone. It is the combination of:

- **lightweight quick-edit behavior**;
- **guarded file writes and destination revalidation**;
- **content-neutral Open and Save**;
- **explicit preservation of encoding, BOM and line endings**;
- **external-change detection without silent reload/adoption**;
- **exact Saved/Modified semantics across Undo and Redo**;
- **crash recovery without turning the editor into a session manager**;
- enough mature desktop features to avoid feeling intentionally incomplete.

## File safety

Graphium treats the state of the file on disk separately from the text visible in the editor.

Saving uses same-directory staging and revalidates the destination before replacement. If another process replaces or changes the file unexpectedly, Graphium fails closed instead of silently overwriting the new filesystem object.

External filesystem notifications are not accepted as authoritative state. They trigger a fresh observation of the file, and unexpected changes are reported to the user.

Graphium does **not** silently normalize a file merely because it was opened and saved. Encoding, BOM, line endings, whitespace and final-newline representation are preserved unless the user explicitly requests a conversion or transformation.

## Features

### Files and safety

- New, Open, Open Recent, Save and Save As
- **Save a Copy**
- **Save Version Copy**
- guarded saving with destination revalidation
- external-file change detection
- explicit **Reload from Disk**
- crash recovery
- UTF-8, UTF-16 and UTF-32
- LF, CRLF and CR line endings
- explicit handling of mixed line endings

### Editing

- Undo / Redo
- Cut / Copy / Paste / Delete / Select All
- Find / Replace
- Go to Line
- Duplicate Line / Selection
- Move Lines Up / Down
- Uppercase / Lowercase
- Trim Trailing Spaces

### View and document controls

- Line Numbers
- Word Wrap
- Font and Zoom
- compact status information
- System / Light / Dark appearance
- direct **Tab Width**
- direct **Insert Spaces Instead of Tabs**
- document statistics
- explicit encoding and line-ending conversion

### Desktop integration

- Hunspell spell checking with selection among installed dictionaries
- GTK Page Setup
- Print Preview
- Print
- offline User Guide
- offline Keyboard Shortcuts
- native Linux desktop entry and application icon

## Editing model

Graphium is intentionally single-document.

To edit several files at the same time, open several Graphium windows. Each invocation owns its own process, window and active document.

Search is literal and limited to the current document. Graphium intentionally does not provide project search, regular expressions, syntax highlighting, tabs or plugins.

## Installation

### Debian / Ubuntu / Linux Mint

Download the current `.deb` from the repository's **Releases** page and install it with:

```bash
sudo apt install ./graphium_<version>_all.deb
```

### Run from source

Requirements:

- Linux
- Python 3
- PyGObject
- GTK 3
- Hunspell (optional, for spell checking)

```bash
git clone https://github.com/leviagravia/graphium.git
cd graphium
./bin/graphium
```

### Install for the current user

```bash
./bin/graphium-install
```

The default installation prefix is `~/.local`.

## Keyboard shortcuts

| Action | Shortcut |
|---|---|
| New | `Ctrl+N` |
| Open | `Ctrl+O` |
| Save | `Ctrl+S` |
| Save As | `Ctrl+Shift+S` |
| Reload from Disk | `F5` |
| Undo | `Ctrl+Z` |
| Redo | `Ctrl+Shift+Z` |
| Find | `Ctrl+F` |
| Find Next | `F3` |
| Find Previous | `Shift+F3` |
| Replace | `Ctrl+H` |
| Go to Line | `Ctrl+G` |
| Uppercase | `Ctrl+U` |
| Lowercase | `Ctrl+Shift+L` |
| Print | `Ctrl+P` |
| Full Screen | `F11` |
| Move Lines Up | `Alt+Up` |
| Move Lines Down | `Alt+Down` |
| Quit | `Ctrl+Q` |

See [`docs/user/GRAPHIUM_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUTS.txt`](docs/user/GRAPHIUM_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUTS.txt) for the complete list.

## Deliberate non-goals

Graphium is not trying to be a smaller IDE.

It deliberately does **not** provide:

- tabs inside one window;
- project/workspace management;
- syntax highlighting;
- language modes;
- column editing;
- plugin marketplace;
- integrated file browser;
- cloud services.

Users who depend on those workflows may be better served by Mousepad, FeatherPad, Kate or a code-oriented editor.

Graphium instead focuses on the quick-edit user who values a conventional interface, low cognitive load and strong trust in Save and file-state behavior.

## Documentation

- [`User Guide`](docs/user/GRAPHIUM_USER_GUIDE.txt)
- [`Keyboard Shortcuts`](docs/user/GRAPHIUM_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUTS.txt)

Both are also available offline from Graphium's **Help** menu.

## About the name

*Graphium* is Latin for a **writing stylus**, the instrument traditionally used to write on wax tablets: a simple tool made for writing.

## License

Graphium is free software released under the **GNU General Public License v3.0 or later (GPL-3.0-or-later)**.

See [`LICENSE`](LICENSE) for the complete license terms.

**leviagravia**  
`leviagravia@zohomail.eu`
