ENTRY — PLUS-S1 CHAT-EMERGED TEST / T480 / CANDIDATE GOVERNANCE CLARIFICATION
Date: 2026-08-28
State: CANONICAL / BINDING / IMMEDIATE EFFECT / APPLIES TO PLUS AND ALL FUTURE GRAPHIIUM WORK
===============================================================================

CONTEXT
-------
During Graphium Plus-S1, two successive NON-CANDIDATE T480 True-GTK runs exposed
qualification-oracle defects before a valid product conclusion could be drawn:

  1. one scenario registered a Core Gtk.Application and then a Plus Gtk.Application
     in the same process, violating the mature one-application-root-per-process
     topology before the Plus window/toolbar was exercised;
  2. the next scenario compared GtkToolButton.get_sensitive() with GAction.enabled,
     while GTK3/Mousepad source proves that GtkToolButton forwards the actionable
     binding to its internal GtkButton child, which is the widget whose sensitivity
     GtkActionHelper updates.

These failures are preserved as evidence that green test counts, fault-injection
counts, or broad coverage are not sufficient proof when the oracle itself does not
own the real product/toolkit boundary. The user therefore froze the following
clarifications. They supplement, and where stricter supersede, prior test/T480
practice. They do not weaken any existing safety, filesystem, GTK, identity,
performance, packaging, Candidate or publication requirement.

A. SANDBOX-FIRST TEST EXECUTION — MANDATORY
-------------------------------------------
  SANDBOX_FIRST_TEST_EXECUTION=MANDATORY
  TEST_MEANINGFULLY_EXECUTABLE_IN_ASSISTANT_SANDBOX_MUST_RUN_THERE=YES
  USER_PC_FOR_SANDBOX_CAPABLE_TEST=FORBIDDEN
  T480_IS_NOT_A_CONVENIENCE_EXECUTION_HOST=YES

Before asking the user to execute any test on the T480/PC, the assistant MUST record:

  1. the exact product/property boundary to be proven;
  2. why that property cannot be tested meaningfully in the assistant sandbox;
  3. the minimal test scope required on the real machine;
  4. why existing sandbox/headless/static evidence is insufficient;
  5. that no broader or redundant T480 lane is being delegated merely for convenience.

Examples of legitimate T480-only boundaries MAY include real GTK/display/session
behavior, installed-desktop integration, real system service/tool integration,
hardware/OS behavior, or packaging/install behavior only when the exact property
cannot be reproduced meaningfully in the sandbox. The category name alone is not
sufficient justification: if the relevant property can be tested meaningfully in
the sandbox, it MUST be tested there and MUST NOT be delegated to the user.

Manual testing remains last-resort evidence. A manual test is forbidden when an
automated sandbox or automated T480 test can prove the same property faithfully.

B. USER-PC / CANDIDATE DELIVERY — SAME-MESSAGE COMMAND IS MANDATORY
-------------------------------------------------------------------
The existing USER-PC COMMAND DELIVERY POLICY remains binding and is strengthened:

  EVERY_USER_PC_TEST_REQUEST_INCLUDES_TERMINAL_COMMAND_SAME_MESSAGE=YES
  TERMINAL_COMMAND_MUST_BE_COMPLETE_COPY_PASTE_READY=YES
  TEST_PACKAGE_SHA256_AND_COMMAND_MUST_BE_DELIVERED_TOGETHER=YES
  NO_USER_PC_TEST_REQUEST_WITHOUT_ITS_EXACT_LAUNCH_COMMAND=YES

Additionally, whenever a Candidate is delivered for the user to download, install,
execute, validate, or keep on the T480/PC, the SAME assistant message MUST include:

  - the exact Candidate package link/name and SHA-256;
  - the complete copy/paste-ready terminal command sequence;
  - the exact extraction/install/run location appropriate to that Candidate;
  - a fail-visible runner/command path that does not silently close on failure;
  - any required instruction for leaving the Candidate installed/present on the PC.

A Candidate package MUST NOT be delivered first with the terminal command deferred
to a later message. If no user-PC execution is required, no terminal command is
invented merely for ceremony.

C. FAIL CLASSIFICATION BEFORE ANY REPAIR / RETRY
------------------------------------------------
  RETRY_UNTIL_GREEN=FORBIDDEN
  BLIND_REISSUE=FORBIDDEN
  PATCH_AND_RETRY_WITHOUT_SOURCE_AUDIT=FORBIDDEN
  FAILURE_CLASSIFICATION_BEFORE_MUTATION=MANDATORY

After any FAIL, STOP before changing product or test code. Determine source-first,
using mature-source comparison where relevant, whether the evidence proves:

  PRODUCT_FAIL
  HARNESS_FAIL
  ORACLE_FAIL
  ENVIRONMENT_BOUNDARY_FAIL/BLOCKED
  PACKAGING/TRANSPORT_FAIL
  or another explicitly justified class.

A wrong oracle MUST NOT be repaired by modifying the product to satisfy it. A
product defect MUST NOT be relabelled as harness/oracle failure merely because a
nearby test assumption was also weak. When both defects coexist, both are recorded.

No automatic T480 retry is permitted. A later T480 execution is allowed only when
it proves a materially corrected or previously untested real boundary, after local
source-first requalification and explicit user authorization. It is not called a
"retry" merely to evade this rule.

D. ANTI-COSMETIC / ANTI-HAPPY-TEST RULE — DEFINITION
----------------------------------------------------
"Cosmetic action" in this governance means an action that makes qualification look
better without making the evidence truer. It does NOT mean ordinary visual-product
work such as fixing GUI layout, icon color/identity, spacing, ordering, contrast or
other user-visible presentation when source/product analysis shows that change is
actually required.

The following qualification-cosmetic actions are FORBIDDEN:

  - weakening an oracle because the product currently fails it;
  - choosing only happy-path fixtures that avoid the discovered failure class;
  - replacing a hostile/negative case with an easier passing case without a
    source-grounded reason;
  - adding parallel tests whose main purpose is to create more green counts;
  - treating BLOCKED/INCOMPLETE/NOT-EXERCISED as PASS;
  - testing implementation literals when the durable semantic owner is elsewhere;
  - adding product-side synchronization, duplicate state, or workaround code solely
    to satisfy a mistaken test observation;
  - using coverage percentage, raw test count, or exact PASS count as a substitute
    for proving the relevant property;
  - retaining known-invalid oracle assumptions because changing them would disturb
    historical counts or make the qualification less convenient.

A test/oracle repair is valid only when source/toolkit/mature evidence proves that
its previous observation boundary was wrong and the repaired oracle observes the
real authority without weakening the intended product guarantee.

E. TEST-VALUE / ANTI-MULTIPLICATION GATE
----------------------------------------
  TEST_COUNT_IS_NOT_A_QUALITY_METRIC=YES
  TEST_LOC_IS_A_PERMANENT_COST=YES
  HARNESS_LOC_IS_A_PERMANENT_COST=YES
  DUPLICATE_PROPERTY_COVERAGE_DEFAULT=REJECT
  GENERIC_TEST_FRAMEWORK_WITHOUT_PROVEN_NEED=REJECT

Before adding a permanent test, scenario, helper, lane or harness abstraction, record:

  1. UNIQUE PROPERTY — what durable property/failure class is not already owned;
  2. REAL AUTHORITY — which product/toolkit/filesystem/runtime owner decides it;
  3. FALSIFIABILITY — what realistic negative mutation/state would make it fail;
  4. NON-DUPLICATION — why an existing Behavioral, Integration, True-GTK Desktop or
     Packaging/Release authority cannot protect the same property;
  5. COST — additional files/LOC/dependencies/runtime and whether existing test
     infrastructure can be reduced or reused instead.

If an existing permanent authority can be strengthened to own the property without
creating a second authority, prefer that. Do not create a new scenario simply to
separate each assertion into its own executable unit. Exact test counts remain
informational/accounting evidence only; they are never an architectural quality
objective.

When a historical/obsolete test can be deleted or simplified after a stronger
owner exists, prefer reduction rather than keeping both for reassurance.

F. PRODUCT / HARNESS ANTI-BLOAT EXTENSION
-----------------------------------------
The Lightweight Budget and GS07 net-reductive governance apply equally to Plus and
Ultra product code and qualification infrastructure.

  UNUSED_GENERICITY=REJECT
  FRAMEWORK_FOR_ONE_FEATURE=REJECT_BY_DEFAULT
  DUPLICATED_METADATA_OR_STATE_AUTHORITY=REJECT
  PRODUCT_LOC_AND_TEST_LOC_MUST_BE_REPORTED_AS_COST=YES
  PASS_COUNTS_DO_NOT_OVERRIDE_BLOAT_FINDINGS=YES

Do not add generic parameters, service layers, worker/job frameworks, compatibility
wrappers, plugin systems, duplicated metadata, duplicated sensitivity/state paths,
or reusable-looking abstractions unless a current demonstrated requirement needs
them. "May be useful later" is insufficient.

A source reduction that removes unnecessary authority/genericity is preferred over
a green test that preserves the bloat. Conversely, LOC reduction MUST NOT be used
to delete essential hostile/safety guarantees; structural simplification remains
invalid if it weakens a real product guarantee.

G. T480 MINIMALITY / EVIDENCE REUSE
-----------------------------------
Valid prior T480 evidence for an unchanged property remains usable historical/current
support when exact source/property identity is preserved. Do not rerun an unchanged
platform boundary merely because another nearby source/test file changed.

A requested T480 lane MUST be the smallest lane that proves the unresolved real
boundary. Full-suite T480 execution is forbidden when the unresolved property is
narrow and the broader suites are already meaningfully executable/proven elsewhere.

The assistant MUST NOT transfer routine CPU/time cost to the user's T480 when the
same evidence can be produced in its sandbox.

H. CANDIDATE / ATTEMPT GOVERNANCE REMAINS SEPARATE
-------------------------------------------------
No NON-CANDIDATE source/test repair, sandbox qualification or platform probe becomes
a Candidate automatically. Candidate construction/declaration and any attempt
consumption remain separately authorized under the existing governance. A harness
or oracle failure before meaningful product execution does not become a product
failure merely to preserve attempt accounting, and attempt accounting must never be
used to soften a real product failure.

BINDING SUMMARY MARKERS
-----------------------
  SANDBOX_FIRST_TEST_EXECUTION=YES
  SANDBOX_CAPABLE_TEST_ON_T480=FORBIDDEN
  T480_BOUNDARY_JUSTIFICATION_REQUIRED=YES
  T480_SCOPE_MUST_BE_MINIMAL=YES
  MANUAL_TEST_LAST_RESORT=YES

  USER_PC_PACKAGE_SHA_COMMAND_SAME_MESSAGE=YES
  CANDIDATE_DOWNLOAD_AND_TERMINAL_COMMAND_SAME_MESSAGE=YES
  CANDIDATE_PC_LOCATION_AND_RUN_COMMAND_REQUIRED=YES

  FAIL_CLASSIFY_SOURCE_FIRST_BEFORE_MUTATION=YES
  RETRY_UNTIL_GREEN=FORBIDDEN
  WRONG_ORACLE_PRODUCT_WORKAROUND=FORBIDDEN

  HAPPY_TEST_COSMETIC_COVERAGE=FORBIDDEN
  GUI_ICON_VISUAL_PRODUCT_REPAIR_WHEN_REAL=ALLOWED
  TEST_COUNT_IS_NOT_QUALITY=YES

  TEST_HARNESS_LOC_COUNTS_AS_BLOAT_COST=YES

  PRODUCT_MUTATION_BY_THIS_ENTRY=NO

ENTRY — GRAPHIIUM PLUS CONSOLIDATED STRUCTURAL AUTHORITY AFTER S0-S3 + GEANY AUDIT
Date: 2026-08-28
State: CANONICAL / BINDING / CONSOLIDATED PLUS STRUCTURAL CONTRACT
===============================================================================

PURPOSE / PRECEDENCE
--------------------
This entry consolidates, in one place, all currently binding Graphium Plus structural
choices established across Plus-S0, Plus-S1, Plus-S2 and the subsequent exact-source
audits of Calamus W79-W84, gedit, Geany, Kate and Cistula. Historical entries remain
valid evidence. Where a later finding refined an earlier DEFER/implementation choice,
this consolidated entry is the current Plus authority.

PRODUCT-LINE TOPOLOGY
---------------------
  PRODUCT_LINE=Graphium_Core_SUBSET_Graphium_Plus_SUBSET_Graphium_Ultra
  PLUS_IS_RUNTIME_FEATURE_FLAG=NO
  ULTRA_IS_RUNTIME_FEATURE_FLAG=NO
  CORE_IMPORTS_PLUS=0
  CORE_IMPORTS_ULTRA=0
  PLUS_IMPORTS_ULTRA=0

Graphium Core remains the frozen single-document native GTK editor. Graphium Plus is
cumulative: Core + compact native toolbar + bounded local Workspace. Graphium Ultra
is later cumulative Plus + Markdown capability. Core startup/runtime must never import
or initialize Plus/Ultra code, scan Workspace files, touch Plus state or start Plus
workers.

PLUS REPOSITORY / IDENTITY / LINEAGE
------------------------------------
  PRODUCT_NAME=Graphium Plus
  REPOSITORY_NAME=graphium-plus
  REPOSITORY_REMOTE=https://github.com/leviagravia/graphium-plus.git
  EXECUTABLE=graphium-plus
  PYTHON_NAMESPACE=graphium_plus
  DESKTOP_APPLICATION_ID=io.github.leviagravia.GraphiumPlus
  APPLICATION_ICON_NAME=io.github.leviagravia.GraphiumPlus
  XDG_NAMESPACE=graphium-plus
  LICENSE=GPL-3.0-or-later
  AUTHOR_COPYRIGHT_LICENSE=INHERIT_FROM_CORE
  PLUS_VERSION=DEFER_UNTIL_RELEASE_VERSIONING

The frozen preferred Git lineage is one-time cumulative lineage from exact public Core
HEAD `8899c94006757c066c88739ff84bf8e1a6cb1b35`. Plus does NOT depend at runtime on a
separately installed Graphium Core package, Calamus or Cistula. No Core submodule,
generic plugin framework, edition registry or Core feature flag is introduced.
Changes inside inherited `graphium/` are permitted only as minimal generic seams with
Core-default equivalent behavior and zero Plus imports.

ICON FAMILY
-----------
Graphium Plus uses the exact Graphium icon geometry, including hand-tuned
16/24/32/48/scalable variants, with colorway change only. Plus dominant identity is
red; S1 qualified the current red palette and geometry identity. Ultra later uses the
same geometry with gold/yellow family color. No new logo family or generic icon
framework.

CORE/PLUS AUTHORITY COUNTS
--------------------------
At Plus runtime:
  ACTIVE_DOCUMENT_AUTHORITY_COUNT=1
  DOCUMENT_REPLACEMENT_LIFECYCLE_COUNT=1
  PHYSICAL_DOCUMENT_WRITER_AUTHORITY_COUNT=1
  CANONICAL_COMMAND_AUTHORITY_COUNT=1
  EDITOR_BUFFER_AUTHORITY_COUNT=1

The inherited Core owners remain authoritative:
  DocumentSession              = sole active-document truth
  FileLifecycleController      = sole New/Open/Close/replacement lifecycle
  GuardedFileWriter            = sole physical document writer
  Graphium command catalog     = sole menu/accelerator/toolbar command authority
  GraphiumTextView/TextBuffer  = sole active editor buffer

No Workspace object may become document identity, save authority, dirty/savepoint
authority or recovery authority.

COMPOSITION / GTK SHELL
-----------------------
S1 established the minimal product-line composition seams:
  - immutable ProductIdentity with Core defaults;
  - XDG namespace injection;
  - application identity/window-factory composition;
  - About/printing reuse rather than duplication;
  - Plus-specific composition under `graphium_plus/`.

The Core window must not be wholesale copied. A small explicit inherited editor-surface
reference is permitted so Plus can compose the left Workspace with the existing editor
without widget-tree heuristics or a generic panel/plugin framework.

PLUS TOOLBAR — CLOSED S1 CONTRACT
---------------------------------
Native GTK3 compact icon toolbar:
  New  Open  Save  |  Undo  Redo  |  Reload  Find

Exact canonical action mapping:
  win.new     -> document-new
  win.open    -> document-open
  win.save    -> document-save
  win.undo    -> edit-undo
  win.redo    -> edit-redo
  win.reload  -> view-refresh
  win.find    -> edit-find

GTK toolbar is projection only. Buttons use GtkActionable/GAction binding. No toolbar
callback owns command behavior and no second sensitivity calculation exists. The real
GTK actionable child owns effective sensitivity per GTK3/Mousepad source. Qualified GUI
order is Menu -> Toolbar -> main content -> Status. Toolbar is defining Plus surface,
visible by default; style/visibility persistence/configuration remains deferred absent
real user need.

WORKSPACE TERMINOLOGY / UI NAME
-------------------------------
The architecture/domain name remains `Workspace` in source and contracts. The former
UI phrase "Writing Workspace" is too long and is no longer structurally required.
Current implementation working label is `Workspace`. Final one-word marketing/UI label
may be changed without changing architecture; no structural contract depends on the
visible label.

WORKSPACE SOURCE AUTHORITY ORDER
--------------------------------
Primary implementation/safety authority order is frozen as:
  1. exact current Graphium/Plus source and Core ownership graph;
  2. Calamus W79 exact source = primary read-only Workspace baseline;
  3. Calamus W80-W84 exact milestone deltas = mutation/safety provenance;
  4. gedit File Browser = primary nested lazy GTK-tree comparator;
  5. Geany File Browser/sidebar = one-directory scan/search/focus/context comparator;
  6. Cistula W03 = simplification/state/mutation comparator;
  7. Kate = selective tree/navigation UX/state comparator;
  8. Mousepad/FeatherPad = lightweight/command/UI-complexity boundary comparators.

No comparator becomes a runtime dependency. Calamus-specific academic features,
Research Panel, bibliography/citations, document families, Source Notes/favourites
coupling, historical Wxx compatibility glue and unrelated lifecycle ownership are
REJECTED from Plus.

WORKSPACE ROOT / LOADING MODEL
------------------------------
  WORKSPACE_ROOT_AUTHORITY_COUNT=1
  ROOT_KIND=ONE_LOCAL_DIRECTORY
  ROOT_SYMLINK=REJECT
  REMOTE_VFS_MOUNT_BROWSER=REJECT
  STARTUP_AUTO_RESTORE_ROOT=NO
  STARTUP_WORKSPACE_SCAN=NO
  WORKSPACE_WATCHER=NO
  WORKSPACE_INDEXER=NO
  WORKSPACE_BACKGROUND_WORKER=NO

The read-only foundation uses a **one-directory-at-a-time synchronous local scanner**.
This is now corroborated by exact Geany `plugins/filebrowser.c` + `src/utils.c`, where
refresh enumerates only the current directory, and by gedit's nested on-expand loading.

Frozen lazy behavior:
  - bind root: validate root, scan immediate visible children only;
  - directory row: expandable without scanning descendants;
  - expand: revalidate current in-root non-symlink directory and scan only its immediate
    children;
  - collapse: zero filesystem operation;
  - Refresh: explicit/manual only; rebuild root and only re-expand paths that were
    already expanded in memory;
  - no arbitrary global item cap copied from Cistula's recursive architecture;
  - no permanent async/job framework unless a measured real directory boundary later
    proves synchronous one-directory enumeration inadequate.

Directory expansion/restoration state is in-memory navigation context only. It is not a
session file and is not persisted across restart.

WORKSPACE ITEM / STALE / CONFINEMENT MODEL
------------------------------------------
Items are immutable and minimal. Required semantic fields:
  root
  path
  relative_path
  name
  is_directory
  is_symlink
  text_document
  generation/current-snapshot identity

Recursive-depth metadata is not carried merely because Calamus/Cistula recursive
snapshots used it. GtkTreeIter/GtkTreePath/QModelIndex equivalents are projection-only,
never filesystem identity.

Ordering:
  directories first -> name.casefold() -> original name

Hidden names beginning with `.` are omitted in the first baseline. No hidden-files
toggle is added.

Symlink policy is fail-closed:
  - symlink root rejected;
  - symlink row may be displayed with a symlink icon;
  - symlink directory never expanded;
  - symlink file never internally or externally activated;
  - expansion/activation rechecks current symlink/type/existence state.

Confinement uses realpath/commonpath revalidation. Root rebinding/Refresh increments
current generation; old row/item objects become stale and cannot act. A failed root
transition preserves the previous valid root/tree. A failed Refresh preserves the
previous successfully rendered authority where possible and reports stale/error state.

WORKSPACE GUI / INTERACTION
---------------------------
Main Plus layout:
  Menu
  Toolbar
  Gtk.Paned(HORIZONTAL)
      left  = Workspace panel
      right = existing inherited Graphium editor scroller
  Status

No second editor/view/buffer/window is created for Workspace.

First read-only panel surface:
  - Open Folder
  - Recent
  - Refresh
  - Reveal
  - bounded root-name/path projection
  - single-selection Gtk.TreeView
  - native GtkTreeView incremental search on **loaded rows only**
  - Enter/double-click activation
  - visible warning/error state

Geany source promoted two details into the first baseline:
  NATIVE_TREE_LOADED_ROW_SEARCH=ADOPT
  EXPANDED_PATH_RESTORE_ON_MANUAL_REFRESH=ADOPT_IN_MEMORY_ONLY

The following remain DEFERRED:
  - follow/auto-select current document;
  - dedicated Workspace focus keybinding;
  - pane-width persistence;
  - show/hide persistence;
  - global filter/search;
  - active-document highlight/locate action;
  - drag/drop and broad context menus.

The following are REJECTED for the first Plus product contract:
  - free path-entry navigation / Up / Home outside the bound root;
  - multi-selection/batch operations;
  - project/session model;
  - tabs/multi-document authority;
  - Find-in-Files/indexed global search;
  - Git UI/terminal/VFS/remote browser.

WORKSPACE SEARCH / FILTER SEMANTICS
-----------------------------------
Geany's native GtkTreeView search is adopted because it acts only on rows already
materialized and requires no global scan/index. A Cistula-style basename filter over the
entire Workspace is NOT copied: with a lazy tree, a truly global filter would require a
recursive scan or index and would undermine the loading model. Therefore:
  LOADED_ROW_INCREMENTAL_SEARCH=YES
  GLOBAL_WORKSPACE_FILTER=DEFER
  GLOBAL_WORKSPACE_SEARCH_INDEX=REJECT_FIRST_BASELINE

DOCUMENT ACTIVATION
-------------------
Editable `.txt`/`.md` activation must follow:
  Workspace row
    -> WorkspaceController activation plan
    -> GraphiumPlusWindow
    -> inherited GraphiumWindow.open_path(path)
    -> external monitor suspend/rebind
    -> canonical FileLifecycleController.open_document(path)
    -> canonical Save/Discard/Cancel guard
    -> existing DocumentSession/editor replacement on success only
    -> Recent Files/projection/focus updates

Workspace must not call DocumentSession, NativeEditorController.initialize_open or
GuardedFileWriter directly. Cancelling unsaved replacement leaves active document
unchanged even if Workspace selection remains selected.

Non-text regular files may delegate explicitly to the desktop through narrow shell-free
Gio default-application activation. Geany's configurable shell command/spawn model is
REJECTED. Reveal delegates only to the selected item's containing directory or current
Workspace root and never becomes document/mutation authority.

RECENT WORKSPACES
-----------------
Recent Workspaces is convenience state distinct from Recent Files:
  RECENT_WORKSPACES_FILE=graphium-plus XDG state/recent-workspaces.json
  RECENT_WORKSPACES_LIMIT=8
  ACTIVE_ROOT_PERSISTED=NO
  DOCUMENT_SESSION_RESTORED=NO

State contains only a schema plus recent root paths. Save uses a private temporary file,
flush/fsync and atomic replace. Missing/symlink/invalid roots are hidden when recents are
requested. Loading is lazy; no Workspace I/O occurs merely because Plus starts.

READ-ONLY STATUS / COUNTS
-------------------------
A lazy tree must never present partial loaded counts as if they were total Workspace
counts. Therefore Calamus W79's whole-snapshot `N files / N folders` status is REJECTED
for the Plus lazy baseline. Only known local status/warnings may be shown.

MUTATION ROADMAP / SAFETY CONTRACT
----------------------------------
Calamus W80-W84 exact deltas are reused as safety provenance but mutations remain serial.

PLUS-S4 — New Text + New Folder
  Shared validation/planning infrastructure is implemented once.
  New Text: because it will become the active document, canonical unsaved/replacement
  preflight occurs **before** file creation; then exclusive creation, postcondition,
  refresh/select and canonical open_path(). User Cancel must not leave an unwanted file.
  New Folder: no document unsaved guard because active document identity is unchanged;
  bounded validation + exactly one creation + postcondition + refresh/select.

PLUS-S5 — Safe Rename
  First baseline allows inactive file and inactive directory only.
  Active document and any ancestor directory containing active document fail closed.
  Pattern: stale/current + confinement validation -> immutable plan -> identity token ->
  one Gio rename -> postcondition -> ownership-safe rollback only when ownership remains
  demonstrable -> refresh/reconciliation.
  Active-document path rebinding is DEFERRED rather than importing Calamus-specific
  lifecycle complexity.

PLUS-S6 — Verified Duplicate
  Regular files only. Source remains active. Duplicate represents bytes saved on disk,
  never unsaved editor buffer, and is not auto-opened. Revalidate source identity/content,
  perform one Gio copy, prove source still same and target content equals source, then
  refresh/select. Workspace never uses the document Save writer for Duplicate.

PLUS-S7 — System Trash
  System Trash only; permanent-delete fallback forbidden. Active document and active-
  document ancestor directory fail closed. Pattern: stale/current + confinement + identity
  checks -> optional Gio capability hint only -> explicit confirmation -> one Gio trash ->
  postcondition -> refresh. `os.remove`, `shutil.rmtree` or equivalent permanent delete is
  forbidden as fallback.

PLUS-S8 — Usability / structural consolidation
  Only after filesystem safety is stable, evaluate active-document highlight/locate,
  show/hide Workspace, pane width and other bounded usability improvements. Geany's
  follow-current-document is a comparator here, not baseline behavior.

PLUS-S9 — Integral qualification / Candidate readiness
  Must be able to FAIL for bloat/duplicate authority even with all functional tests green.
  Audit Core isolation, authority counts, startup I/O, lazy behavior, mutations, hostile
  filesystem cases, dependency/LOC/test-harness growth and absence of Calamus/Cistula
  runtime coupling. Candidate remains separately authorized.

TEST / BLOAT GOVERNANCE FOR PLUS
--------------------------------
The previously appended sandbox-first / anti-happy-test rules remain binding. In
particular:
  - any meaningful sandbox-capable test runs in assistant sandbox, not T480;
  - T480 requires a property that cannot meaningfully be proved in sandbox plus explicit
    justification and minimal scope;
  - no retry-until-green;
  - wrong oracle must be repaired from source truth, never by product workaround;
  - test count is not quality;
  - no multiplication of tests/harnesses for reassurance;
  - test/harness LOC counts as bloat cost;
  - unused genericity / generic framework for one feature is rejected;
  - real GUI/icon/layout repair is permitted and required when source/user evidence shows
    a real defect; "cosmetic" prohibition means masking evidence, not legitimate visual
    product work.

CANDIDATE / USER-PC DELIVERY
----------------------------
Candidate remains separately authorized. Whenever a Candidate/package intended to be
kept/run on the user's PC is delivered, the same assistant message must include the
artifact identity/SHA and complete copy-paste terminal command. A sandbox-capable test
must never be delegated to the PC for convenience.

  PLUS-S2 = CLOSED source-first Workspace contract freeze
  PLUS-S3 = read-only lazy Workspace implementation/current work

  PLUS-S6 = Verified Duplicate
  PLUS-S7 = System Trash
  PLUS-S8 = usability + structural consolidation

  Candidate R1 = separate authorization only

ENTRY — GRAPHIUM PLUS-S3 TRUE-GTK VALID PRODUCT FAIL / LAZY-TREE SOURCE REPAIR
Date: 2026-08-28
State: CANONICAL HANDOVER UPDATE / S3 OPEN / REPAIR LOCALLY QUALIFIED / GTK PROOF PENDING
===============================================================================

USER DIRECTION / GOVERNANCE
---------------------------
After the single authorized S3 True-GTK run failed, the user explicitly prohibited
trial-and-error repair and required renewed mature-source analysis. No retry, reissue or
oracle weakening was performed before that audit. The user subsequently authorized the
source-derived next step and required that explanations lead to a concrete artifact.

OBSERVED TRUE-GTK FAIL
----------------------
The real T480 run reached the Plus Workspace Gtk.TreeView and failed after expanding a
non-empty directory:

  GtkTreeView.expand_row("Chapter")
  -> lazy materialization
  -> GtkTreeView.row_expanded("Chapter") == FALSE

Classification:
  PLUS_S3_PRODUCT_FAIL=YES
  FAIL_AREA=GTK_LAZY_TREE_EXPANSION
  TEST_ORACLE=KEEP
  ORACLE_FAIL=NO
  CANDIDATE=NO
  ATTEMPT_CONSUMED=NO
  RETRY_BEFORE_SOURCE_AUDIT=NO
  PLUS_S4=BLOCKED

MATURE-SOURCE ROOT CAUSE
------------------------
The failed S3 implementation deleted the directory's sole placeholder child before
appending the real loaded children. That forced an expanding GtkTreeView directory
through a transient zero-child state. GTK can destroy the expanded child subtree when
the sole child is deleted; appending rows later does not restore the expansion.

gedit File Browser is the decisive mature comparator for this exact class of problem:
a hierarchical GTK filesystem tree with lazy directory materialization and structural
dummy children. Calamus W79-W84 and Cistula remain primary safety/state provenance but
are not authorities for this transition because they render already-materialized
recursive snapshots. Geany remains authoritative for the simple one-directory scan
primitive and canonical editor handoff, but its file browser uses a flat current-
directory model and therefore does not define hierarchical lazy expansion semantics.

SOURCE-DERIVED REPAIR CONTRACT
------------------------------
For a loaded non-empty directory:
  1. keep the existing placeholder row alive;
  2. mutate that exact row in place into the first real WorkspaceItem;
  3. if that first item is an expandable directory, add its own placeholder child;
  4. append remaining items;
  5. never cross `1 child -> 0 children -> N children` when N > 0.

For a genuinely empty directory only, remove the placeholder so the directory correctly
becomes non-expandable.

No `connect_after`, manual expand forcing, sensitivity/state workaround, custom TreeModel,
async loader, worker, watcher or additional authority is introduced merely to make the
test green. The permanent True-GTK oracle remains byte-identical.

REPAIR DELTA / ANTI-BLOAT
-------------------------

  CHANGED_PATH=graphium_plus/adapters/gtk/workspace_panel.py
  WORKSPACE_PANEL_LOC_DELTA=+10
  PERMANENT_TRUE_GTK_ORACLE_CHANGED=NO
  NEW_PERMANENT_TESTS=0
  NEW_HARNESS=0
  NEW_RUNTIME_AUTHORITY=0
  NEW_THREAD_PROCESS_WATCHER_INDEX_DB=0

-----------------------

AUTHORIZATION / SCOPE

The user explicitly authorized the next serial step after the real-GTK S3 repair PASS.
S4 combines Calamus W80 New Text and W81 New Folder in one work item to avoid duplicating
planner/runtime/qualification infrastructure for two closely related creation actions.

PRIMARY SOURCE AUTHORITY
------------------------
Exact Calamus W80/W81 source is the primary mutation provenance. Cistula is a secondary
commit-boundary/safety comparator. Geany master does not provide a comparable New File /
New Folder mutation implementation in its core file-browser plugin and therefore is not
used as unsupported mutation authority.

GRAPHIIUM-SPECIFIC REPLACEMENT PERMIT
-------------------------------------
Calamus resolves unsaved changes before mutation and then calls an unguarded host
`open_path()`. Graphium's canonical `open_path()` deliberately contains its own guarded
FileLifecycle Open. A direct transplant would therefore double-prompt after DISCARD.

S4 freezes one narrow Core seam:
  `prepare_document_replacement(action_label)` resolves SAVE/DISCARD/CANCEL once and
  returns a `DocumentReplacementPermit` bound to the exact `DocumentSession.revision`.
  `open_document(..., replacement_permit=permit)` accepts it only if the revision is
  unchanged; any intervening document mutation makes the permit stale and Open fails
  closed. `GraphiumWindow.open_path(..., replacement_permit=permit)` remains the canonical
  monitor/recent/projection gateway.

This permit is not a second lifecycle authority, does not discard buffer state itself,
and cannot make filesystem mutation precede Cancel resolution.

NEW TEXT CONTRACT
-----------------
  DESTINATION=selected_directory OR selected_file_parent OR workspace_root
  DESTINATION_SYMLINK=REJECT
  NAME=ONE_BASENAME_ONLY
  HIDDEN_RESERVED_NAME=REJECT
  ALLOWED_SUFFIX=.txt|.md
  FILE_CREATE=Gio.File.create(FileCreateFlags.NONE)
  OVERWRITE=NO
  PARENT_REVALIDATION_AT_COMMIT=YES
  POST_COMMIT_REGULAR_NON_SYMLINK_WITHIN_ROOT=REQUIRED
  UNSAVED_REPLACEMENT_PERMIT=BEFORE_MUTATION
  OPEN_AFTER_CREATE=GraphiumWindow.open_path_with_current_permit
  DOUBLE_UNSAVED_PROMPT=FORBIDDEN
  STALE_PERMIT=FAIL_CLOSED

NEW FOLDER CONTRACT
-------------------
  DESTINATION=same_bounded_creation_destination
  NAME=ONE_BASENAME_ONLY
  HIDDEN_RESERVED_NAME=REJECT
  CREATE=Gio.File.make_directory
  RECURSIVE_MKDIR=NO
  POST_COMMIT_DIRECTORY_NON_SYMLINK_WITHIN_ROOT=REQUIRED
  UNSAVED_GUARD=NO
  OPEN_AS_DOCUMENT=NO

RECONCILIATION
--------------
After a committed creation the Workspace refreshes, restores expanded in-memory paths,
expands the destination parent only when needed to reveal the new item, and selects the
new item. GtkTreeIter remains projection-only and never filesystem identity.

PLUS-S5 SAFE RENAME — SOURCE-FIRST CONTRACT
-------------------------------------------
Primary source authorities re-audited before implementation:
  1. exact Calamus W82 Workspace source;
  2. Cistula W03 mutation/operations/GIO source;
  3. exact current Graphium Plus S4 source and Graphium document identity/lifecycle owners.

Geany master File Browser has no direct rename implementation to transplant. Kate's richer
rename/file operations are delegated through KDE/KIO and remain outside the lightweight
Plus architecture.

S5 deliberately does NOT import Calamus W82's active-document/path-store/Source-Notes
reconciliation. The first Plus rename baseline is bounded:

  INACTIVE_REGULAR_FILE_RENAME=ALLOW
  INACTIVE_DIRECTORY_RENAME=ALLOW
  ACTIVE_DOCUMENT_RENAME=REJECT_FAIL_CLOSED
  ACTIVE_DOCUMENT_ANCESTOR_DIRECTORY_RENAME=REJECT_FAIL_CLOSED
  SYMLINK_RENAME=REJECT
  WORKSPACE_ROOT_RENAME=REJECT
  STALE_FOREIGN_SELECTION=REJECT
  PATH_ESCAPE=REJECT
  SAME_NAME_NOOP=REJECT
  EXISTING_DESTINATION=REJECT_NO_OVERWRITE

Active-document comparison uses the accepted canonical document path when available,
otherwise the logical path. No unsaved-document gate is invoked: a rename capable of
changing active document identity is rejected before filesystem commit.

S5 MUTATION TOPOLOGY
--------------------
  current Workspace selection
    -> generation/current-item validation
    -> symlink/type/root confinement revalidation
    -> active-document/ancestor fence
    -> lstat device/inode/mode token
    -> immutable same-parent WorkspaceRenamePlan
    -> commit-time parent/source/token/collision revalidation
    -> exactly one Gio.File.set_display_name()
    -> returned path + source disappearance + target token/type/symlink/confinement proof
    -> ownership-safe rollback only while target still carries original token
    -> Workspace refresh + deterministic target selection

Forbidden for S5:
  os.rename
  shutil.move
  shell mutation
  active-document lifecycle rebind
  second document/session/save authority
  Source Notes/favorites/recent-file path rewrite imported from Calamus
  worker/thread/watcher/index/database

Existing `workspace.state` use of os.replace/os.unlink remains solely the atomic temporary
Recent Workspaces state-file protocol and is not item-rename authority.

------------------------
No new runtime module or framework was created. Existing owners are extended:

  graphium_plus/adapters/gtk/window.py

The Workspace UI gains one `Rename Selected Item` action with `document-edit-symbolic`.
The dialog is input-only; filesystem mutation remains in the GIO adapter.

================================================================================
2026-08-28 — GRAPHIUM PLUS S5 VALID T480 PRODUCT FAIL / RENAME IDENTITY RE-AUDIT
================================================================================

Binding status update:
- PLUS_S5 remains OPEN after a valid NON-CANDIDATE T480 product-safety FAIL.
- The failing property is the planned-source replacement fence for Safe Rename.
- No retry, reissue, product patch, Candidate, attempt, or Git mutation was performed after the FAIL.

Root cause:
- S5 adopted `WorkspacePathToken(st_dev, st_ino, st_mode)` from Calamus W82/Cistula Rename.
- Linux may immediately reuse a freed inode after unlink+recreate; therefore a different filesystem lifetime can reproduce the same weak token.
- Graphium Core already documents `(dev, ino)` as object evidence, never content identity, and uses stronger observation signatures plus descriptor/path rebinding for file safety.

Binding source-first decision:
- ADOPT from Calamus/Cistula: immutable plan, commit-time revalidation, one GIO rename, fail-closed collision handling, ownership-gated rollback topology.

Calamus/Cistula remain valuable for the bounded product behavior:

However, the earlier S6 wording "perform one Gio copy" is SUPERSEDED. After the S5 inode-reuse finding,
path-only Gio copy plus post-hoc token verification is not strong enough to prove ownership of a target
under the Graphium safety contract. Graphium Core's guarded-write descriptor model is the stronger mature
precedent and is ADOPTED for S6.

S6 VERIFIED DUPLICATE COMMIT CONTRACT
-------------------------------------
- regular files only; directories and symlinks are rejected;
- all regular Workspace files are eligible, not only .txt/.md;
- duplicate bytes are the SAVED ON-DISK source bytes, never unsaved editor-buffer bytes;
- source document/session remains active and its dirty state is unchanged;
- duplicate is not auto-opened;
- deterministic same-parent target name; no overwrite;
- source plan carries the strong S5 signature:
  dev, ino, mode, size, mtime_ns, ctime_ns, uid, gid, nlink;
- commit opens source with O_NOFOLLOW and keeps its descriptor pinned;
- containing directory is descriptor-pinned;
- bytes are streamed into an exclusive hidden sibling stage;
- stage is fsync'd and SHA-256 read-back verified;
- source signature/path binding is revalidated after the copy and immediately before commit;
- final target is committed no-overwrite by same-directory hard link, then the hidden stage name is removed;
- final target identity is proven against the still-open stage descriptor;
- rollback/deletion is allowed only while the pathname still binds that descriptor-owned object;
- no DocumentCopyService/GuardedFileWriter Save authority is reused as the Workspace Duplicate authority;
- no worker/job framework is introduced unless a measured product need later proves one necessary.

----------------------

  dev, ino, mode, size, mtime_ns, ctime_ns, uid, gid, nlink;

S7 SAFETY / SCOPE CONTRACT
--------------------------
- Workspace root itself: REJECT;
- symlink: REJECT;
- only regular files and directories: ALLOW;
- active document: REJECT;
- directory containing active document: REJECT;
- no Save/Discard guard is invoked because permitted Trash never targets active-document scope;
- no restore command is implemented; restore remains system-Trash responsibility;
- no internal application Trash directory;
- no `os.remove`, `os.unlink`, `os.rmdir`, `shutil.rmtree` or equivalent permanent-delete fallback
  exists in the S7 Trash operation;
- confirmation defaults to Cancel and explicitly states that Graphium Plus will not permanently delete
  the item as fallback;
- if Gio accepts Trash but original-path absence cannot be verified, the result is fail-visible and
  Workspace refreshes instead of claiming success.

EXPLICIT GIO API LIMIT
----------------------
`Gio.File.trash()` is a pathname-based API and exposes no fd-bound atomic Trash commit. S7 therefore
performs the strongest practical precommit fence available within the frozen Gio-only Trash contract:
strong signature + O_NOFOLLOW descriptor pin + pathname/descriptor revalidation immediately before the
single `trash()` call, with the descriptor kept open through the call. This detects stale replacements
before commit and prevents freed-inode reuse from impersonating the planned object while pinned.

S7 MUST NOT claim a mathematically impossible atomic identity guarantee across the final pathname race
window inside the Gio API. This residual platform/API boundary is documented rather than hidden by a
happy test or weakened oracle.

S7 UI / RECONCILIATION
----------------------
Workspace adds one `Move Selected Item to Trash` button with `user-trash-symbolic`.
The real product confirmation dialog:
- is modal;
- defaults to Cancel;
- offers only Cancel / Move to Trash;
- states that the operation uses system Trash and has no permanent-delete fallback.

After Gio reports an accepted Trash, Workspace refreshes. A successful root-level removal clears tree
selection; a nested removal selects the surviving parent when possible. The active document/session/dirty
buffer remain unchanged because active-document scope is blocked before confirmation.

PLUS-S8 MATURE-SOURCE FINDINGS
------------------------------
S8 re-audited the already supplied mature sources with emphasis on workspace usability rather than
filesystem mutation:
- Kate File Tree implements an explicit `Show Active Document` action that selects, scrolls to and
  expands the active document ancestry, and shows/focuses the tool view. This is the closest mature
  precedent for Plus's bounded manual locate operation.
- Kate File Browser also supports automatic synchronization with the active document, but that behavior
  is optional. Geany File Browser likewise has an optional `fb_follow_path` document-activate hook.
- Plus REJECTS automatic follow in the first baseline because a lazy bounded Workspace would otherwise
  perform navigation/filesystem loading merely because the editor's active document changes. User intent
  remains explicit.
- gedit's historical `Set root to active document` changes the browser root. Plus REJECTS that model:
  the explicitly bound one-root Workspace remains authority and active-document navigation must never
  silently rebind it.

- Geany provides a dedicated focus-file-list command; Plus does not add a new shortcut in S8 because
  accelerator namespace expansion is unnecessary when manual Locate already transfers focus to the tree.

- manual `Locate Active Document` only;

  an excessively wide sidebar minimum.

REJECT / DEFER:

- active root rebinding from the document: REJECT;
- pane width persistence: DEFER; no new UI state store in S8;
- show/hide persistence: DEFER; no startup state/I/O added;

  needed directory, then selects/scrolls/focuses the active file.

======================================================================
2026-08-28 — GRAPHIIUM PLUS S8 TRUE-GTK FOCUS FAIL: MATURE-SOURCE REAUDIT + FOCUS-PRESERVATION REPAIR
======================================================================

OBSERVED T480 FAIL
------------------
The first S8 True-GTK usability proof reached the manual Locate behavior, successfully expanded the
collapsed ancestor and selected the active document, then failed only at:
  `assert window.get_focus() is window.workspace_panel.tree`
No rerun was performed before mature-source re-audit.

MANDATORY POST-FAIL MATURE-SOURCE FINDINGS
------------------------------------------
- Kate File Tree `showActiveDocument()` calls `viewChanged()` to select/scroll/expand the active document,
  then shows/focuses the generic tool view. Its file selection routine does not establish an exact
  `QTreeView` focus contract.
- Kate Project `selectFile()` likewise scrolls and selects without calling `setFocus()` on the tree.
- Geany File Browser separates navigation/follow-current-document from focus ownership: its
  `document_activate_cb()` changes directory/refreshes without grabbing the file list, while a separate
  `KB_FOCUS_FILE_LIST` command explicitly calls `gtk_widget_grab_focus(file_view)` when focus transfer is
  actually requested.
- gedit keeps document focus as an explicit primary concern around panel visibility; its mature panel
  behavior does not establish 'selection in a side panel implies exact tree focus' as an invariant.

ROOT CAUSE / CONTRACT CORRECTION
--------------------------------
S8 had invented an overly specific focus contract: `Locate Active Document` was required to transfer
focus exactly to the Workspace `Gtk.TreeView`. The mature sources do not converge on that property.
Graphium Plus already sets Workspace action buttons `focus-on-click=False`, which expresses the opposite
editor-first policy: navigation actions should not steal typing focus unnecessarily. The additional
`self.tree.grab_focus()` in `WorkspacePanel.locate_relative_path()` contradicted that policy.

The corrected Graphium Plus contract is therefore:
- Locate Active Document reveals the bounded Workspace if needed;
- lazily expands only required ancestors;
- selects and scrolls the active file into view;
- DOES NOT change root;
- DOES NOT auto-follow future document changes;
- DOES NOT steal the editor's existing keyboard focus.
A dedicated future 'Focus Workspace' command may be considered only if a real user need appears; it is
not part of S8.

REPAIR
------
Product repair is deliberately reductive:
- delete the single `self.tree.grab_focus()` call from `WorkspacePanel.locate_relative_path()`.
No other product owner changes.

The existing external S8 True-GTK proof is corrected rather than weakened:
- it explicitly establishes the real `GraphiumTextView` as focus before Locate;
- after Locate it requires the same editor focus to remain;
- it repeats this preservation check when Locate reveals a previously hidden Workspace.
The test still requires ancestor expansion, selection, visibility and no-auto-follow, so no functional
coverage is removed.

ENTRY — GRAPHIUM PLUS-S9 INTEGRAL QUALIFICATION / STRUCTURAL CONSOLIDATION / CANDIDATE READINESS

PURPOSE
-------
S9 is deliberately not a functional-PASS counting exercise. It audits the exact cumulative Plus line for
Core isolation, duplicated authorities, startup I/O, dead/test-only runtime API, dependency growth,
Workspace loading/mutation boundaries, packaging identity, user documentation and test/harness growth. It is
permitted to fail even when all functional suites are green.

INITIAL S9 SOURCE-PROVEN BLOCKERS
---------------------------------
The first S9 audit found three real pre-Candidate deficiencies despite S1-S8 product functionality being
closed:

1. `PLUS_PRODUCT_IDENTITY.version` was still `None`; a Candidate would not have an independent release
   identity.
2. Plus had a source launcher and icons but no own installed desktop projection: no Plus `.desktop` file and
   no Plus installer. A separate-product Candidate must not depend on running from the source tree.
3. `WorkspaceController` retained five runtime API surfaces with no product consumer: `generation`,
   `root_listing`, `listing_for`, `current_item`, and `clear`. Four existed only as test/introspection
   convenience and one was completely dead. Keeping them merely because tests were green violates the
   binding anti-bloat/test-value policy.

These are Candidate-readiness defects, not S1-S8 feature regressions.

S9 CONSOLIDATION / REMEDY
-------------------------
The exact S8 source was consolidated without reopening any completed Workspace feature:

- Graphium Plus begins its own unpublished early-stage release line at `0.0.1`. This is not a semantic
  stability claim and does not alter Core version `0.0.16`.
- additive `bin/graphium-plus-install` installs one private `/lib/graphium-plus` runtime containing inherited
  `graphium/` + additive `graphium_plus/`, one public `graphium-plus` symlink, Plus desktop file, Plus icon
  family, user docs and GPL license. It installs no Core public launcher or Core desktop entry.
- additive `graphium_plus/data/io.github.leviagravia.GraphiumPlus.desktop` freezes the independent desktop
  identity: `Name=Graphium Plus`, `Exec=graphium-plus %F`, Plus application icon and no terminal.
- additive `docs/user/GRAPHIUM_PLUS_USER_GUIDE.txt` is a complete independent Plus guide: inherited Core
  behavior plus a bounded Workspace section. Plus Help -> User Guide projects this guide; keyboard-shortcut
  help remains the inherited shortcut content but is titled Graphium Plus in the Plus window.
- the five no-product-consumer `WorkspaceController` APIs above are removed. Existing tests are rewritten to
  use controller return values or the actual GTK tree projection rather than forcing test-only runtime getters.
  No new test file/framework is introduced and no product behavior is weakened.

RELEASE / INSTALL IDENTITY
--------------------------
  PLUS_VERSION=0.0.1_UNPUBLISHED
  PRODUCT_NAME=Graphium Plus
  EXECUTABLE=graphium-plus
  INSTALLER=graphium-plus-install
  DESKTOP_APPLICATION_ID=io.github.leviagravia.GraphiumPlus
  DESKTOP_FILE=io.github.leviagravia.GraphiumPlus.desktop
  XDG_NAMESPACE=graphium-plus
  USER_GUIDE=GRAPHIUM_PLUS_USER_GUIDE.txt
  LICENSE=GPL-3.0-or-later

The sandbox staged installer proves the Plus public projection independently and does not install
`/usr/bin/graphium` or `io.github.leviagravia.Graphium.desktop`. The same installer also succeeds under a HOME
path containing spaces and creates `~/.local/bin/graphium-plus` as the public symlink.

2026-08-28 — GRAPHIIUM PLUS CANDIDATE R1 USER-VISIBLE FAIL + POST-R1 REPAIR CONTRACT
================================================================================

Observed Candidate R1 platform smoke:
- installed projection: PASS
- runtime smoke: PASS
- candidate remained installed at /home/luciano/Applications/Graphium-Plus-Candidate-R1

User-visible findings after smoke:
1. the compact icon toolbar cannot be hidden, while toolbar visibility is required to be optional;
2. Reveal in File Manager opens the containing/current folder rather than requesting the desktop file manager to reveal the selected item.

Classification:
- CANDIDATE_R1=RETIRED_VALID_PRODUCT_FAIL
- ATTEMPT_1_OF_2=CONSUMED
- ATTEMPT_2_OF_2=REMAINS
- CANDIDATE_R2=NOT_DECLARED; requires separate explicit authorization
- runtime smoke PASS does not override a user-visible product FAIL.

Mature-source audit:
- Geany implements toolbar visibility as a user-controlled View-menu state and persists it.
- historical gedit likewise has an explicit toolbar-visible View setting.
- Graphium Plus adapts only the essential property now: View > Toolbar is a stateful presentation toggle; persistence is deliberately DEFERRED to avoid introducing new session state solely for this corrective.
- Kate uses KIO::highlightInFileManager({url}) for "open containing folder" semantics that reveal/highlight the exact selected URL rather than merely launching dirname.
- Linux desktop interoperability authority is org.freedesktop.FileManager1: ShowItems for selected item(s), ShowFolders for a folder location.

Post-R1 repair contract:
- View > Toolbar MUST hide/show the compact Plus toolbar in the current window.
- hiding the toolbar MUST NOT disable canonical menu commands or shortcuts.
- toolbar visibility in 0.0.1 corrective remains transient; no new persistent setting is introduced.
- Workspace Reveal with a selected item MUST request FileManager1.ShowItems for that exact item's URI.
- with no Workspace item selected, Reveal MUST request FileManager1.ShowFolders for the Workspace root.
- if FileManager1 is unavailable, fallback may open the relevant directory, but Graphium Plus MUST NOT claim that the exact item was highlighted.
- Candidate R1 source behavior "always launch dirname" is retired.
- Nemo-specific behavior is not an authority for Graphium: current Nemo versions may mishandle ShowItems for directories; file-item reveal must be judged using a selected regular file.

Test policy:
- no new permanent test file/scenario is created for this corrective;
- existing plus_toolbar and plus_workspace scenarios are extended with the new properties;
- any stale static oracle that scans unrelated callbacks must be narrowed to the owner/property it actually protects rather than forcing product code to satisfy it.
